<tr><td colspan="7">例 The youngest hiker lagged far behind the group on the steep trail.  最年幼的徒步者在陡峭的小道上远远落在队伍后面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 linger</td><td>/ˈlɪŋɡər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>磨蹭，闲荡</td><td>linger in the doorway<br>在门口徘徊磨蹭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guests lingered in the doorway long after the party had officially ended.  聚会正式结束后很久，客人们还在门口磨蹭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loiter</td><td>/ˈlɔɪtər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>闲荡，徘徊</td><td>loiter near the entrance<br>在入口附近闲荡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Teenagers loitered outside the shop until the owner finally complained.  少年们在店外闲荡，直到店主终于抱怨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tarry</td><td>/ˈtæri/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>逗留，耽搁</td><td>tarry no longer<br>不再逗留</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not tarry here; the last train leaves in minutes.  别在这儿磨蹭，末班车几分钟后就开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bolt</td><td>/ˈboʊlt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>突然说出；猛冲</td><td>bolt out an answer<br>脱口说出答案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He bolted out the secret before anyone could stop him.  他还没等人阻止就把秘密说了出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hasten</td><td>/ˈheɪsn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加速，促进；催促</td><td>hasten the decision<br>促使尽快决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Warm weather hastened the melting of snow on the high mountain passes.  暖和的天气促进了高山山口积雪的融化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 course</td><td>/ˈkɔːrs/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>奔流，快速行进</td><td>course through his veins<br>在他血管中奔流</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Adrenaline coursed through his veins as the race finally began.  比赛终于开始时肾上腺素在他血管中奔涌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dash</td><td>/ˈdæʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>猛冲，疾奔</td><td>dash across the street<br>快速冲过街道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She dashed across the street to catch the departing bus.  她冲过街道去赶那辆即将开走的公交车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 speed</td><td>/ˈspiːd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>快速行进，疾驰</td><td>speed down the highway<br>在公路上疾驰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ambulance sped down the empty highway toward the hospital.  救护车沿着空旷的公路疾驰驶向医院。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scurry</td><td>/ˈskɜːri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>急匆匆地跑</td><td>scurry for cover<br>急忙找掩护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Mice scurried across the floor when the light came on.  灯一亮，老鼠们急匆匆跑过地板。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>deadpan</b></td><td>/ˈdedpæn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不带表情的，板着脸的</td><td>a deadpan expression<br>面无表情的神情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He told the joke with a deadpan face and nobody laughed.  他面无表情地讲了那个笑话，没有人笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expressionless</td><td>/ɪkˈspreʃənləs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无表情的，木然的</td><td>an expressionless stare<br>木然的凝视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His expressionless face gave no hint of what he was thinking.  他毫无表情的脸没有透露他在想什么。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impassive</td><td>/ɪmˈpæsɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷漠的，无感情的</td><td>remain impassive under pressure<br>压力下仍无动于衷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His face stayed impassive as the verdict was read aloud.  宣读判决时，他的脸上始终毫无表情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inexpressive</td><td>/ˌɪnɪkˈsprɛsɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无表情的，缺乏表现力的</td><td>an inexpressive monotone voice<br>缺乏表现力的单调声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her inexpressive reading of the poem bored the entire audience.  她毫无表现力的朗诵让全场听众感到乏味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stolid</td><td>/ˈstɑːlɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无动于衷的，感情麻木的</td><td>a stolid unsmiling guard<br>麻木不笑的卫兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A stolid unsmiling guard stood beside the gate all afternoon.  一名麻木不笑的卫兵整个下午守在门旁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vacant</td><td>/ˈveɪkənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>茫然的，无表情的；空的</td><td>a vacant expression<br>茫然的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered with a vacant stare that unsettled everyone present.  他以茫然的目光作答，让在场的人都不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 demonstrative</td><td>/dɪˈmɑːnstrətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>感情外露的</td><td>a demonstrative greeting<br>热情外露的问候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her demonstrative hugs surprised colleagues used to formal handshakes.  她热情的拥抱让习惯正式握手的同事很惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 expressive</td><td>/ɪkˈspresɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>富于表情的，善于表达的</td><td>expressive dark eyes<br>富有表情的黑眼睛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dancer's expressive gestures told the whole story without any words.  舞者富有表现力的动作无需言语便讲完整个故事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dearth</b></td><td>/dɜːrθ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>供应不足</td><td>a dearth of talent<br>人才匮乏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a real dearth of affordable housing in this district.  这个区确实缺乏买得起的住房。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 want</td><td>/ˈwɑːnt/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>缺乏，短缺</td><td>for want of evidence<br>因缺乏证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The project collapsed for want of steady funding and clear leadership.  项目因缺乏稳定资金和明确领导而失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deficit</td><td>/ˈdefəsət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>不足，亏空</td><td>a severe water deficit<br>严重的水资源不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The region faces a serious water deficit after three dry years.  该地区在三个干旱年后面临严重缺水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insufficiency</td><td>/ˌɪnsəˈfɪʃənsiː/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不足，不充分</td><td>insufficiency of evidence<br>证据不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The case was dismissed because of the insufficiency of the evidence.  由于证据不足，案件被驳回。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 paucity</td><td>/ˈpɔːsəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>少量，缺乏</td><td>a paucity of data<br>数据匮乏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paucity of reliable data made the researchers cautious about conclusions.  可靠数据的匮乏使研究者对结论十分谨慎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pinch</td><td>/ˈpɪntʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>短缺，窘迫；捏</td><td>feel the pinch<br>感到经济窘迫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Small shops began to feel the pinch as rents rose sharply.  租金猛涨后小店铺开始感到经济窘迫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 scantiness</del></td><td>/ˈskæntinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不足，稀少</td><td>the scantiness of rainfall<br>降雨稀少</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scantiness of the harvest worried farmers across the whole valley.  收成稀少让整个山谷的农民忧心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scarcity</td><td>/ˈskersɪti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>稀缺，不足</td><td>a scarcity of housing<br>住房稀缺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A scarcity of clean water threatens millions of people every year.  清洁水源的稀缺每年威胁数百万人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 undersupply</del></td><td>/ˌʌndərsəˈplaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>供应不足</td><td>an undersupply of nurses<br>护士供应不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A chronic undersupply of nurses has strained the country's hospitals.  护士长期供应不足使全国医院不堪重负。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 plethora</td><td>/ˈpleθərə/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>过量，过剩</td><td>a plethora of options<br>过多的选项</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The menu offers a plethora of options that confuse first time visitors.  菜单提供过多的选项，让初次光顾的客人不知所选。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 spate</td><td>/speɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大量</td><td>a spate of complaints<br>大量投诉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The store received a spate of complaints about the faulty heaters.  商店收到大量关于取暖器故障的投诉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 copiousness</del></td><td>/ˈkoʊpiəsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>丰富，充裕</td><td>the copiousness of detail<br>细节丰富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The copiousness of his notes impressed even the strictest professor.  他笔记的丰富程度连最严格的教授都佩服。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abundance</td><td>/əˈbʌndəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>丰富，大量</td><td>an abundance of fresh fruit<br>大量新鲜水果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market offered an abundance of fresh fruit at low prices.  市场以低价供应大量新鲜水果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adequacy</td><td>/ˈædəkwəsi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>充足，适当</td><td>the adequacy of funding<br>资金是否充足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Inspectors questioned the adequacy of the building's fire safety measures.  检查员质疑该建筑消防措施是否充足。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amplitude</td><td>/ˈæmpləˌtuːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>充裕，广大</td><td>amplitude of resources<br>资源充裕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library's amplitude of rare manuscripts attracts scholars from many countries.  该图书馆丰富的珍稀手稿吸引了许多国家的学者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 opulence</td><td>/ˈɑːpjələns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>富裕，丰裕</td><td>live in sheer opulence<br>生活极度奢华</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The palace displayed an opulence that stunned every visiting guest.  这座宫殿展现出令每位来客震惊的奢华。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>debacle</b></td><td>/deɪˈbɑːkl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>溃败</td><td>an economic debacle<br>经济上的溃败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The campaign ended in a debacle that cost the party three seats.  这场竞选以溃败收场，使该党丢掉三个席位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bummer</td><td>/ˈbʌmər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>令人失望的事，扫兴的事</td><td>what a bummer<br>真扫兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Losing the tickets an hour before the show was a real bummer.  演出前一小时丢了票，真是件扫兴的事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 calamity</td><td>/kəˈlæməti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>灾难，大祸；不幸事件</td><td>a national calamity<br>国家性的大灾难</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The flood was the worst calamity the small town had faced.  这场洪水是这个小镇遭遇过的最严重灾难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 catastrophe</td><td>/kəˈtæstrəfi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大灾难；彻底的失败</td><td>an ecological catastrophe<br>生态大灾难</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The oil spill became an ecological catastrophe for the whole coast.  漏油事件成为整个海岸的生态大灾难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cataclysm</td><td>/ˈkætəklɪzəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大灾难；剧变</td><td>a social cataclysm<br>社会剧变</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The eruption was a cataclysm that reshaped the entire island.  那次喷发是一场重塑整座岛屿的大灾难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fiasco</td><td>/fiˈæskoʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大失败</td><td>end in a fiasco<br>以惨败告终</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poorly planned launch party ended in a complete fiasco.  筹划糟糕的发布会以彻底的失败告终。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fizzle</td><td>/ˈfɪzəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>失败，虎头蛇尾地结束</td><td>fizzle out<br>逐渐失败告终</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The protest fizzled out after the organizers lost their funding.  组织者失去资金后，抗议活动渐渐无声收场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flop</td><td>/ˈflɑːp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>失败，惨败</td><td>a box-office flop<br>票房惨败之作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The expensive musical turned out to be a complete flop.  这部昂贵的音乐剧结果彻底失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 washout</td><td>/ˈwɑːˌʃaʊt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>彻底的失败；失败者</td><td>a complete washout<br>彻头彻尾的失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rainy festival turned out to be a complete washout.  那个下雨的音乐节结果彻底失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 complete success</td><td>/kəmˈpliːt səkˈsɛs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>完全成功</td><td>prove a complete success<br>证明是完全成功</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rescue operation was a complete success despite the terrible weather.  尽管天气恶劣，救援行动完全成功。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 blockbuster</td><td>/ˈblɑːkbʌstər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>非常成功的作品，大片</td><td>a summer blockbuster<br>暑期大片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The studio needed a blockbuster to cover last year's losses.  制片厂需要一部大片来弥补去年的亏损。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>debark</b></del></td><td>/diˈbɑːrk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）下船（飞机、车等）；卸（客、货）</td><td>debark from the ship<br>从船上下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The passengers debarked slowly onto a windy and unfamiliar wooden pier.  乘客们缓缓下船，走上一个多风而陌生的木码头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 land</td><td>/ˈlænd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>着陆，登陆；上岸</td><td>land safely on the runway<br>安全降落在跑道上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plane landed safely despite strong crosswinds at the airport.  尽管机场有强侧风，飞机仍安全降落。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 embark</td><td>/ɪmˈbɑːrk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>开始从事</td><td>embark on a new career<br>开始新的职业生涯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At forty she embarked on a new career as a literary translator.  四十岁时她开始从事文学翻译这一新职业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>debase</b></td><td>/dɪˈbeɪs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，贬损</td><td>debase the currency<br>使货币贬值</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Politicians who lie to win votes debase the office they hold.  为拉票而撒谎的政客贬损了他们所担任的职务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abase</td><td>/əˈbeɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>降低（地位、威望、尊严）</td><td>abase oneself before someone<br>在某人面前自贬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He would not abase himself by begging for the job.  他不愿低声下气地乞求这份工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debauch</td><td>/dɪˈbɔtʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使堕落，腐化</td><td>debauch the young<br>腐化年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics claimed the film would debauch impressionable young viewers.  批评者称这部电影会腐蚀易受影响的年轻观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 degrade</td><td>/dɪˈɡreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，使降级；使退化</td><td>degrade the quality<br>降低质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such insulting jokes degrade everyone who repeats them.  这类侮辱性的笑话贬低了每个转述它们的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demean</td><td>/dɪˈmiːn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低，降低尊严</td><td>demean oneself<br>自贬身份</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to demean his colleagues in front of clients.  他拒绝在客户面前贬低同事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demoralize</td><td>/dɪˈmɔːrəlaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使士气低落；贬低，使堕落</td><td>demoralize the troops<br>使部队士气低落</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repeated pay cuts demoralized the staff more than the long hours.  反复减薪比长时间工作更让员工士气低落。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 deprave</del></td><td>/diˈpreɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使道德败坏，腐化</td><td>deprave public morals<br>腐化公众道德</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics claimed such films would deprave impressionable young viewers.  批评者称这类影片会腐化易受影响的年轻观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deteriorate</td><td>/dɪˈtɪriəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>恶化，变坏</td><td>deteriorate rapidly<br>迅速恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her health deteriorated sharply during the cold winter.  在寒冷的冬天里她的健康急剧恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 profane</td><td>/prəˈfeɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>亵渎</td><td>profane a sacred place<br>亵渎圣地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vandals profaned the temple by scrawling slogans on its walls.  破坏者在庙墙上乱涂标语，亵渎了圣殿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subvert</td><td>/səbˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>颠覆</td><td>subvert the government<br>颠覆政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The group was accused of plotting to subvert the elected government.  该团体被指控阴谋颠覆经选举产生的政府。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vitiate</td><td>/ˈvɪʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>削弱，损害；使无效</td><td>vitiate an agreement<br>使协议失效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single false statement can vitiate the entire contract.  一句虚假陈述就能使整份合同失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aggrandize</td><td>/əˈɡrændaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扩大（权力、财富、声望）</td><td>aggrandize one's power<br>扩张自己的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor used the crisis to aggrandize his own power.  市长利用危机扩大自己的权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 canonize</td><td>/ˈkænənaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使神圣，封为圣徒</td><td>canonize a saint<br>封为圣徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The church voted to canonize the nun a century after her death.  教会在这位修女去世百年后决定将她封为圣徒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deify</td><td>/ˈdeɪɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>神化，奉若神明</td><td>deify a leader<br>神化领袖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime tried to deify its founder in every textbook.  该政权试图在每本教科书里神化其创始人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 elevate</td><td>/ˈelɪveɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提升，提高；使兴奋</td><td>elevate the mind<br>提升心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good literature can elevate the mind and refine one's taste.  优秀的文学能提升心智、陶冶品味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exalt</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːlt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞扬；提升（地位）</td><td>exalt to the skies<br>推崇到极点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics exalt her early novels as masterpieces of the era.  评论家将她早期的小说赞为那个时代的杰作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ennoble</td><td>/ɪˈnoʊbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使尊贵，使高尚</td><td>ennoble the spirit<br>使精神高尚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Suffering, he believed, could ennoble an ordinary life.  他相信苦难能使平凡的人生变得高贵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 5

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>debilitate</b></td><td>/dɪˈbɪlɪteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>debilitate the immune system<br>使免疫系统衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The virus gradually debilitates the immune system of healthy adults.  这种病毒会逐渐削弱健康成年人的免疫系统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 devitalize</del></td><td>/diːˈvaɪtəlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使失去活力</td><td>devitalize the local economy<br>使当地经济失去活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of neglect devitalized the once thriving downtown district.  多年的忽视使曾经繁荣的市中心失去活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 enervate</del></td><td>/ˈenərveɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>enervate the whole team<br>使全队疲弱无力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long heat wave enervated the whole team before the final match.  漫长的热浪在决赛前使全队疲弱无力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 enfeeble</del></td><td>/ɪnˈfiːbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱，使虚弱</td><td>enfeeble the patient<br>使病人虚弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long fever enfeebled him so much that walking hurt.  长期发烧使他虚弱到走路都疼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prostrate</td><td>/ˈprɑːstreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>平躺的，俯卧的；使极度虚弱</td><td>lie prostrate on the ground<br>平躺在地上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The exhausted runner lay prostrate on the ground after crossing the line.  那名精疲力竭的跑者冲线后平躺在地上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sap</td><td>/sæp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>削弱，逐渐耗尽</td><td>sap his strength<br>削弱他的力量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Weeks of night shifts sapped the energy of the young doctor completely.  数周的夜班彻底削弱了这位年轻医生的精力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 invigorate</td><td>/ɪnˈvɪɡəreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使精神，使强壮，鼓舞激励</td><td>invigorate the local economy<br>振兴当地经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk in cold air always invigorates my tired mind.  在冷空气中短暂散步总能让我疲惫的头脑重新振作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fortify</td><td>/ˈfɔːrtɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加固；增强，强化</td><td>fortify a city wall<br>加固城墙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The milk is fortified with extra vitamin D.  这种牛奶额外强化添加了维生素D。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 strengthen</td><td>/ˈstreŋθən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>加强，增强</td><td>strengthen the economy<br>增强经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise will strengthen your heart and lungs.  规律锻炼会增强你的心肺功能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 beef up</td><td>/biːf ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>加强，充实</td><td>beef up security measures<br>加强安保措施</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city beefed up security before the international summit began.  国际峰会开始前，该市加强了安保。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>debris</b></td><td>/dəˈbriː/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>碎片，残骸</td><td>clear away the debris<br>清理废墟碎片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rescuers dug through the debris for hours looking for survivors.  救援人员在废墟中挖掘了数小时，寻找生还者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dross</td><td>/ˈdrɔːs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>渣滓，无价值之物</td><td>separate gold from dross<br>从渣滓中分出黄金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most of the manuscripts were dross, but two showed real talent.  大多数稿件都是垃圾，但有两篇显出真才华。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dust</td><td>/dʌst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>灰尘，尘土</td><td>a thick layer of dust<br>厚厚的一层灰尘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A thick layer of dust covered every book on the shelf.  书架上每本书都覆盖着厚厚的灰尘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 litter</td><td>/ˈlɪtər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>垃圾，杂物</td><td>pick up the litter<br>捡起垃圾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Volunteers picked up litter along the riverbank all Saturday morning.  志愿者整个周六上午沿河岸捡垃圾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 offal</td><td>/ˈɔːfəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>内脏；废弃物</td><td>a pile of offal<br>一堆内脏废料</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The butcher sold the offal cheaply to local sausage makers.  屠夫把内脏廉价卖给当地香肠作坊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refuse</td><td>/rɪˈfjuːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>拒绝，不肯；废弃物</td><td>refuse to cooperate<br>拒绝合作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness refused to answer any further questions in court.  证人拒绝在法庭上回答任何进一步的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 effluvium</td><td>/ɪˈfluːviəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>难闻的气味</td><td>a foul effluvium<br>难闻的臭气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A foul effluvium rose from the ditch behind the abandoned tannery.  废弃制革厂后面的沟里升起难闻的臭气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 junk</td><td>/dʒʌŋk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>废旧杂物，垃圾</td><td>a garage full of junk<br>满是废物的车库</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He finally cleared the junk out of his crowded garage.  他终于把拥挤车库里的废物清理掉了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>debunk</b></td><td>/ˌdiːˈbʌŋk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭穿…的真面目</td><td>debunk the myth<br>揭穿这个神话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single careful experiment debunked the theory that had misled everyone.  一次严谨的实验揭穿了那个误导所有人的理论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 uncloak</del></td><td>/ʌnˈkloʊk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭露，揭开真相</td><td>uncloak a conspiracy<br>揭露阴谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The journalist worked for months to uncloak the corruption scheme.  这位记者花了数月揭露这个腐败阴谋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncover</td><td>/ənˈkʌvər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭开，发现，揭露</td><td>uncover the evidence<br>发现证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Inspectors uncovered evidence of fraud in the accounting records.  检查人员在账目中发现了欺诈的证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 undress</td><td>/ʌnˈdres/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>脱衣；使显露</td><td>undress before the mirror<br>在镜子前脱衣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She undressed quickly and slipped into the warm bath.  她迅速脱掉衣服，滑进温暖的浴缸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unmask</td><td>/ʌnˈmæsk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭露，揭穿真面目</td><td>unmask the impostor<br>揭穿那个冒充者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The journalist unmasked the company's secret polluting practices.  记者揭露了这家公司秘密排污的做法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 show up</td><td>/ʃoʊ ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>揭露，暴露；出现</td><td>show up his ignorance<br>暴露他的无知</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The simple test showed up serious flaws in their argument.  这个简单的检验暴露了他们论证的严重缺陷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 camouflage</td><td>/ˈkæməflɑːʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>伪装，掩饰；伪装物</td><td>military camouflage<br>军事伪装</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soldiers used branches and mud as camouflage in the forest.  士兵们用树枝和泥浆在森林里作伪装。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cloak</td><td>/kloʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>遮掩，隐藏</td><td>cloak the truth<br>掩盖真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials cloaked the truth behind pages of confusing statistics.  官员用一堆令人困惑的统计数字掩盖真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disguise</td><td>/dɪsˈɡaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>伪装，掩饰；伪装</td><td>disguise his intentions<br>掩饰他的意图</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He disguised his disappointment behind a polite and steady smile.  他用礼貌平静的微笑掩饰了失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mask</td><td>/ˈmæsk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>掩盖，掩饰；面具</td><td>mask his anger<br>掩饰他的怒气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud music masked the sound of the approaching helicopter.  响亮的音乐掩盖了直升机靠近的声音。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>debut</b></td><td>/deɪˈbjuː/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>初次登台，出道</td><td>make her stage debut<br>首次登台演出</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pianist made her professional debut at only sixteen years old.  这位钢琴家年仅十六岁就首次职业登台。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 farewell performance</td><td>/ˌfɛrˈwɛl pərˈfɔːrməns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>告别演出</td><td>give a farewell performance<br>举行告别演出</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The aging soprano gave her farewell performance to a weeping audience.  这位年迈的女高音在观众的泪水中举行告别演出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>decadence</b></td><td>/ˈdekədəns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>衰落，颓废</td><td>signs of moral decadence<br>道德颓废的迹象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The historian blamed the empire's fall on decades of quiet decadence.  这位历史学家把帝国的覆亡归因于数十年的悄然衰颓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 degeneracy</td><td>/dɪˈdʒenərəsi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>堕落，退化</td><td>moral degeneracy<br>道德堕落</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel portrays the slow degeneracy of a once noble family.  小说描绘了一个曾经高贵家族的缓慢堕落。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 degeneration</td><td>/dɪˌdʒenəˈreɪʃn/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>退化，衰退</td><td>the degeneration of nerve cells<br>神经细胞退化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors monitored the gradual degeneration of the patient's eyesight.  医生监测着病人视力的逐渐衰退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 degradation</td><td>/ˌdeɡrəˈdeɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>退化，恶化；堕落</td><td>environmental degradation<br>环境退化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rapid environmental degradation has already destroyed much of the wetland.  快速的环境退化已破坏了大部分湿地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deterioration</td><td>/dɪˌtɪriəˈreɪʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恶化；堕落</td><td>a sharp deterioration in health<br>健康急剧恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors noted a sharp deterioration in the old man's health.  医生注意到这位老人的健康急剧恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 downfall</td><td>/ˈdaʊnˌfɔːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>垮台，衰落</td><td>lead to his downfall<br>导致他垮台</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Greed eventually led to the powerful banker's sudden downfall.  贪婪最终导致这位有权势的银行家突然垮台。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eclipse</td><td>/ɪˈklɪps/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使声望下降，使黯然失色</td><td>eclipse his earlier fame<br>使他早年的名声黯然</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His later failures eclipsed the fame he had earned as a young pianist.  他后来的失败使他年轻时作为钢琴家赢得的名声黯然失色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ascent</td><td>/əˈsent/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>上升，攀登</td><td>a steady ascent to power<br>稳步登上权力顶峰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her steady ascent to power surprised many longtime party members.  她稳步登上权位让许多老党员感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rise</td><td>/ˈraɪz/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>上升，兴起</td><td>the rise of an empire<br>一个帝国的兴起</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians still debate the rise of that short-lived empire.  历史学家仍在争论那个短命帝国的兴起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 upswing</td><td>/ˈʌpswɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>上升，好转</td><td>an upswing in the economy<br>经济好转</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Retailers reported a welcome upswing in sales last quarter.  零售商报告上季度销售出现可喜的回升。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>detest</b></td><td>/dɪˈtest/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>厌恶，憎恨</td><td>detest hypocrisy<br>厌恶虚伪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I detest waiting in long lines on hot days.  我讨厌在炎热的日子里排长队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abhor</td><td>/əbˈhɔːr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>极度厌恶，深恶痛绝</td><td>abhor violence<br>痛恨暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She abhors any form of cruelty to animals.  她极度厌恶任何形式的虐待动物行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 abominate</del></td><td>/əˈbɑːmɪneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>痛恨，厌恶</td><td>abominate cruelty<br>憎恶残忍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He abominated the hypocrisy he saw all around him.  他痛恨周围随处可见的虚伪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dislike</td><td>/dɪsˈlaɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>不喜欢，厌恶</td><td>dislike crowded places<br>讨厌拥挤的地方</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He dislikes crowded places and avoids the subway during rush hour.  他讨厌拥挤的地方，高峰时段避开地铁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hate</td><td>/ˈheɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>憎恨，厌恶</td><td>hate being lied to<br>讨厌被人骗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hates being lied to, even about very small things.  她讨厌被骗，哪怕是很小的事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loathe</td><td>/loʊð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>厌恶，讨厌</td><td>loathe the sight of<br>一见就厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He loathes the smell of boiled cabbage.  他讨厌煮卷心菜的味道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>decipher</b></td><td>/dɪˈsaɪfər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>破译，解读</td><td>decipher an ancient script<br>破译古代文字</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scholars finally deciphered the inscription after comparing it with three others.  学者们把这段铭文与另外三段比对后终于将其破译。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 break</td><td>/ˈbreɪk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>损坏，打破；间歇</td><td>break the vase<br>打破花瓶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cat broke the vase while chasing a moth indoors.  猫在屋里追蛾子时打破了花瓶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crack</td><td>/ˈkræk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>破解，破译</td><td>crack the enemy's code<br>破解敌方密码</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Mathematicians finally cracked the enemy's code after months of work.  数学家们经过数月努力终于破解了敌方密码。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decrypt</td><td>/diːˈkrɪpt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解密，破译</td><td>decrypt the encrypted file<br>解密加密文件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only the recipient's private key can decrypt this sensitive file.  只有收件人的私钥才能解密这个敏感文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decode</td><td>/ˌdiːˈkoʊd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解码，解读</td><td>decode the secret message<br>解读密信</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took the analysts a week to decode the intercepted message.  分析人员花了一周时间才解读出截获的电文。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apprehend</td><td>/ˌæprɪˈhend/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>逮捕；理解</td><td>apprehend the suspect<br>逮捕嫌疑人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police apprehended the thief two streets from the shop.  警方在离商店两条街的地方逮住了小偷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perceive</td><td>/pərˈsiːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>察觉，理解</td><td>perceive a subtle change<br>察觉细微的变化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She perceived a subtle change in his tone during dinner.  晚餐时她察觉到他语气的微妙变化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recognize</td><td>/ˈrekəɡˌnaɪz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>认出，辨识</td><td>recognize a familiar face<br>认出熟悉的面孔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He recognized her familiar handwriting on the faded old envelope.  他在褪色的旧信封上认出她熟悉的字迹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 seize</td><td>/ˈsiːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>领会，抓住（要点）</td><td>seize the main point<br>抓住要点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good students quickly seize the main point of a difficult lecture.  好学生能迅速抓住难懂讲座的要点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sense</td><td>/ˈsens/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>意思，含义；感觉</td><td>in a broad sense<br>广义上讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In a broad sense, every artist borrows from earlier traditions.  广义上讲，每位艺术家都借鉴前人的传统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 make out</td><td>/meɪk ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>辨认出，看清</td><td>make out the faint writing<br>辨认出模糊的字迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I could barely make out the faint writing on the label.  我几乎看不清标签上模糊的字迹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cipher</td><td>/ˈsaɪfər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>密码</td><td>break the cipher<br>破解密码</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Codebreakers took months to break the enemy cipher.  破译人员花了几个月才破解敌方的密码。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 encipher</del></td><td>/ɪnˈsaɪfər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加密，编成密码</td><td>encipher a military order<br>将军令加密</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 encode</td><td>/ɪnˈkoʊd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>编码，加密</td><td>encode the data securely<br>安全地编码数据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The app encodes your password before storing it on the server.  该应用在把密码存到服务器前先进行编码。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 encrypt</td><td>/enˈkrɪpt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加密</td><td>encrypt sensitive emails<br>加密敏感邮件</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 decent</td><td>/ˈdiːsənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>得体的，正派的</td><td>decent and respectful behavior<br>得体而恭敬的举止</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 respectable</td><td>/rɪˈspektəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>体面的，值得尊敬的</td><td>a respectable middle-class family<br>体面的中产家庭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They came from a respectable family with an excellent local reputation.  他们出身于当地声誉极佳的体面家庭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 seemly</td><td>/ˈsiːmli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>得体的，合宜的</td><td>seemly conduct in public<br>公共场合得体的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher expects seemly conduct from her students in public places.  老师期望学生在公共场所举止得体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 improper</td><td>/ˌɪmˈprɑːpər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不适当的，不合礼节的</td><td>improper use of funds<br>资金使用不当</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was dismissed for improper conduct toward a colleague.  他因对同事行为不当而被解雇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inappropriate</td><td>/ˌɪnəˈproʊpriət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不适当的，不合适的</td><td>inappropriate behavior at work<br>工作中的不当行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wearing shorts to the interview was clearly inappropriate.  穿短裤去面试显然不合适。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indecent</td><td>/ɪnˈdiːsnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不得体的，下流的</td><td>indecent language in public<br>公开场合的下流言语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The comedian was fined for using indecent language on live television.  这位喜剧演员因在直播电视上使用下流言语被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indecorous</td><td>/ɪnˈdekərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不合乎礼节的，不得体的</td><td>indecorous remarks at the ceremony<br>典礼上不得体的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His indecorous jokes at the memorial service embarrassed nearly everyone in the room.  他在追悼会上不得体的玩笑让在场几乎所有人都很尴尬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indelicate</td><td>/ɪnˈdelɪkət/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不雅的，粗俗的</td><td>an indelicate question<br>不雅的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Asking about her salary was an indelicate question at the party.  在聚会上问她的薪水是个不得体的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unseemly</td><td>/ʌnˈsiːmli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不得体的，不适宜的</td><td>unseemly haste<br>失礼的匆忙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their unseemly quarrel spoiled the whole wedding reception.  他们不得体的争吵毁了整场婚宴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>decrepit</b></td><td>/dɪˈkrepɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的，衰老的，破旧的</td><td>a decrepit old building<br>一栋破旧的老楼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They lived for years in a decrepit house with no running water.  他们在一栋没有自来水的破旧房子里住了多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vigorous</td><td>/ˈvɪɡərəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力旺盛的，强有力的</td><td>vigorous exercise<br>剧烈运动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors recommend thirty minutes of vigorous exercise daily.  医生建议每天进行三十分钟的高强度运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sturdy</td><td>/ˈstɜːrdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强健的，结实的</td><td>a sturdy wooden table<br>结实的木桌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sturdy wooden table stood in the middle of the farmhouse kitchen.  农舍厨房中间摆着一张结实的木桌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sound</td><td>/saʊnd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>牢固的；健康强壮的；（逻辑）严谨的</td><td>sound financial advice<br>稳妥的理财建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lawyer gave sound financial advice that saved the family thousands.  她的律师给出稳妥的理财建议，为家里省下数千元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 robust</td><td>/roʊˈbʌst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的；强壮的，健康的</td><td>robust good health<br>强健的好身体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At eighty he remains robust enough to work in the garden daily.  他八十岁了，身体依然强健，每天还能在园中劳作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hale</td><td>/heɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强壮的，健壮的</td><td>hale and hearty<br>身体强健，精神饱满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At eighty he remains hale enough to walk five miles a day.  八十岁的他依然强健，每天能走五英里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 6

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>default</b></td><td>/dɪˈfɔːlt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>不履行义务，玩忽职守</td><td>default on a loan<br>拖欠贷款不还</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company's default on its loans alarmed investors across the region.  这家公司拖欠贷款不还，使全地区的投资者惊慌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delinquency</td><td>/dɪˈlɪŋkwənsi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>失职；违法行为</td><td>juvenile delinquency<br>青少年违法行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Social workers study why juvenile delinquency rises in poorer neighborhoods.  社工研究为何贫困社区的青少年违法行为增多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dereliction</td><td>/ˌderəˈlɪkʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>失职，怠忽</td><td>dereliction of duty<br>失职，怠忽职守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was dismissed for a serious dereliction of duty.  该官员因严重失职被解职。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 misprision</td><td>/mɪsˈprɪʒən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>知情不报；失职</td><td>misprision of a felony<br>对重罪知情不报</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was charged with misprision of a felony he witnessed.  他因对目睹的重罪知情不报而被起诉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 nonfeasance</del></td><td>/nɑːnˈfiːzəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不作为，失职</td><td>nonfeasance by a public official<br>公职人员的不作为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawsuit alleged nonfeasance by several city safety inspectors.  诉讼指控几名市安检员不作为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 oversight</td><td>/ˈoʊvərsaɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>疏忽，失察；监管</td><td>due to a clerical oversight<br>因文书疏忽</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Your name was omitted through a simple clerical oversight.  你的名字因一个小小的文书疏忽被遗漏了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>defer</b></td><td>/dɪˈfɜːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>推迟，延期；遵从</td><td>defer the final decision<br>推迟最终决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee deferred its decision until the new figures finally arrived.  委员会推迟决定，直到新数据终于送来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delay</td><td>/dɪˈleɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>延迟，推迟</td><td>delay the departure<br>推迟出发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy fog delayed the flight by more than four hours.  大雾使航班延误了四个多小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remit</td><td>/rɪˈmɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>减轻，免除；汇款</td><td>remit a penalty<br>免除罚款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge agreed to remit part of the heavy fine.  法官同意减免部分高额罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shelve</td><td>/ˈʃelv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>搁置，推迟</td><td>shelve the ambitious plan<br>搁置宏大计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company shelved the ambitious plan after funding fell through.  资金落空后公司搁置了这个宏大计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hold off</td><td>/hoʊld ˈɔːf/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>推迟，拖延</td><td>hold off the announcement<br>推迟宣布</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Let's hold off the announcement until the contract is signed.  我们把宣布推迟到合同签好之后吧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hold over</td><td>/hoʊld ˈoʊvər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>延期，留待以后</td><td>hold over the vote<br>把表决延期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee held over the vote until next month's meeting.  委员会把表决延到下月的会议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lay over</td><td>/leɪ ˈoʊvər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>延期；中途停留</td><td>lay over the hearing<br>把听证会延期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge laid over the hearing to allow more evidence.  法官延期听证以便收集更多证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 put off</td><td>/pʊt ˈɔːf/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>推迟，拖延</td><td>put off the meeting<br>推迟会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't put off the dentist appointment any longer than necessary.  牙医预约别再往后拖了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acquiescence</td><td>/ˌækwiˈesəns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>默许，顺从</td><td>silent acquiescence to authority<br>对权威的默默顺从</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her silent acquiescence surprised colleagues who expected a fierce argument.  她默默顺从让预料会激烈争论的同事很惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 compliancy</del></td><td>/kəmˈplaɪənsi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>顺从，依从</td><td>expect complete compliancy<br>期望完全顺从</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager expected total compliancy from every member of staff.  经理期望每位员工完全顺从。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 docility</td><td>/dɑˈsɪləti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>温顺，顺从</td><td>the docility of the herd<br>畜群的温顺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The docility of these horses makes them ideal for beginners.  这些马的温顺使它们非常适合初学者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obedience</td><td>/oʊˈbiːdiəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>服从，顺从</td><td>blind obedience to orders<br>对命令的盲目服从</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Blind obedience to orders cannot excuse such cruel behavior.  盲目服从命令不能为如此残酷的行为开脱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 submissiveness</td><td>/səbˈmɪsɪvnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>顺从，恭顺</td><td>a habit of submissiveness<br>顺从的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her submissiveness in meetings hid a very sharp critical mind.  她在会上的顺从掩盖了极为敏锐的批判头脑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 contempt</td><td>/kənˈtempt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>蔑视，鄙视</td><td>treat with open contempt<br>公开表示蔑视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He treated their careful proposal with open and unmistakable contempt.  他对他们精心的提案表现出公开而明显的蔑视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 defiance</td><td>/dɪˈfaɪəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>违抗，公然反抗</td><td>in open defiance of orders<br>公然违抗命令</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The students marched in open defiance of the new ban.  学生们游行，公然违抗新的禁令。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disobedience</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈbiːdiəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不服从，违抗</td><td>civil disobedience<br>非暴力反抗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Civil disobedience became their main weapon against the unjust law.  非暴力反抗成了他们对抗不公法律的主要武器。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intractability</td><td>/ˌɪntræktəˈbɪləti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>倔强，难驾驭</td><td>the intractability of the dispute<br>争端难以解决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The intractability of the dispute frustrated every experienced mediator involved.  争端的难解让每位经验丰富的调解人都很沮丧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 recalcitrance</td><td>/rɪˈkælsətrəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>顽抗，不服从</td><td>stubborn recalcitrance toward reform<br>对改革的顽固抵抗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The department's recalcitrance blocked every attempt at meaningful reform.  该部门的顽抗阻挡了每次有意义的改革尝试。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 imperious</td><td>/ɪmˈpɪriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，专横的</td><td>an imperious tone<br>专横的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her imperious manner made junior colleagues afraid to disagree.  她专横的态度让年轻同事不敢有异议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impudent</td><td>/ˈɪmpjədənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>放肆大胆的，无礼的</td><td>an impudent remark<br>一句无礼的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The impudent student argued with the professor in class.  这个无礼的学生在课上与教授争辩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>document</b></td><td>/ˈdɑːkjument/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>记录，证明</td><td>document the findings carefully<br>仔细记录以证实发现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team documented every finding carefully in a detailed field notebook.  团队把每项发现都仔细记录在详尽的野外笔记本里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demonstrate</td><td>/ˈdemənstreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（通过证据）证明，表明</td><td>demonstrate the theory clearly<br>清楚地证明该理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The experiment demonstrated the theory clearly to every skeptical observer.  实验向每位持疑的观察者清楚证明了该理论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prove</td><td>/ˈpruːv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>证明，证实</td><td>prove his innocence<br>证明他无罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New evidence finally proved his innocence after eleven long years.  新证据在漫长的十一年后终于证明他无罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 establish</td><td>/ɪˈstæblɪʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>确证，证实；建立</td><td>establish the facts beyond doubt<br>确证事实无疑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The investigation established the facts beyond any reasonable doubt.  调查毫无疑问地确证了事实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 substantiate</td><td>/səbˈstænʃieɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实</td><td>substantiate the claim<br>证实这一说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No evidence has emerged to substantiate his serious accusations.  没有证据能证实他严重的指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 validate</td><td>/ˈvælədeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，确认；使有效</td><td>validate the results<br>验证这些结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Independent labs validated the results within three months.  独立实验室在三个月内验证了这些结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disprove</td><td>/ˌdɪsˈpruːv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>反驳，证明为假</td><td>disprove the theory<br>驳倒这一理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Magellan's voyage finally disproved the belief in a flat earth.  麦哲伦的航行最终驳倒了地球是平的这一信念。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rebut</td><td>/rɪˈbʌt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>反驳，驳斥</td><td>rebut the accusation<br>反驳这项指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She rebutted every charge with documents and dated photographs.  她用文件和标注日期的照片反驳了每项指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 refute</td><td>/rɪˈfjuːt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>驳倒，反驳</td><td>refute the claim<br>驳斥这一说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scientist used new data to refute the claim about rising temperatures.  这位科学家用新数据反驳了关于气温上升的说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>deficiency</b></td><td>/dɪˈfɪʃnsi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>缺乏，不足</td><td>a vitamin deficiency<br>维生素缺乏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A serious iron deficiency left her tired and short of breath.  严重缺铁使她疲倦、气短。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 want</td><td>/ˈwɑːnt/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>缺乏，短缺</td><td>for want of evidence<br>因缺乏证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The project collapsed for want of steady funding and clear leadership.  项目因缺乏稳定资金和明确领导而失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dearth</td><td>/dɜːrθ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>供应不足</td><td>a dearth of talent<br>人才匮乏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a real dearth of affordable housing in this district.  这个区确实缺乏买得起的住房。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deficit</td><td>/ˈdefəsət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>不足，亏空</td><td>a severe water deficit<br>严重的水资源不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The region faces a serious water deficit after three dry years.  该地区在三个干旱年后面临严重缺水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 famine</td><td>/ˈfæmən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>饥荒，严重缺乏</td><td>widespread famine and disease<br>大范围饥荒与疾病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Drought brought widespread famine to the region for three years.  干旱使该地区连续三年发生大范围饥荒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 paucity</td><td>/ˈpɔːsəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>少量，缺乏</td><td>a paucity of data<br>数据匮乏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paucity of reliable data made the researchers cautious about conclusions.  可靠数据的匮乏使研究者对结论十分谨慎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 scantiness</del></td><td>/ˈskæntinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不足，稀少</td><td>the scantiness of rainfall<br>降雨稀少</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scantiness of the harvest worried farmers across the whole valley.  收成稀少让整个山谷的农民忧心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scarcity</td><td>/ˈskersɪti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>稀缺，不足</td><td>a scarcity of housing<br>住房稀缺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A scarcity of clean water threatens millions of people every year.  清洁水源的稀缺每年威胁数百万人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 surfeit</td><td>/ˈsɜːrfɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>（使）过量，（使）饮食过度</td><td>a surfeit of information<br>信息过量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A surfeit of information often leaves readers more confused than informed.  信息过量常让读者更困惑而非更明白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abundance</td><td>/əˈbʌndəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>丰富，大量</td><td>an abundance of fresh fruit<br>大量新鲜水果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market offered an abundance of fresh fruit at low prices.  市场以低价供应大量新鲜水果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adequacy</td><td>/ˈædəkwəsi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>充足，适当</td><td>the adequacy of funding<br>资金是否充足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Inspectors questioned the adequacy of the building's fire safety measures.  检查员质疑该建筑消防措施是否充足。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amplitude</td><td>/ˈæmpləˌtuːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>充裕，广大</td><td>amplitude of resources<br>资源充裕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library's amplitude of rare manuscripts attracts scholars from many countries.  该图书馆丰富的珍稀手稿吸引了许多国家的学者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 opulence</td><td>/ˈɑːpjələns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>富裕，丰裕</td><td>live in sheer opulence<br>生活极度奢华</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The palace displayed an opulence that stunned every visiting guest.  这座宫殿展现出令每位来客震惊的奢华。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sufficiency</td><td>/səˈfɪʃənsi/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>充足，足量</td><td>a sufficiency of food<br>食物充足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The harvest guaranteed a sufficiency of grain for the entire winter.  这次收成保证了整个冬天粮食充足。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>devious</b></td><td>/ˈdiːviəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>狡猾的</td><td>devious tactics<br>狡诈的手段</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He used devious means to obtain a copy of the secret contract.  他用狡猾的手段弄到了那份秘密合同的副本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beguiling</td><td>/bɪˈɡaɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迷人的；有欺骗性的</td><td>a beguiling but false promise<br>诱人却虚假的承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His beguiling manner hid a thoroughly calculating and selfish nature.  他迷人的举止掩盖了极其精于算计的自私本性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crafty</td><td>/ˈkræfti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>狡猾的，诡计多端的</td><td>a crafty politician<br>狡猾的政客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A crafty lawyer found a loophole nobody else had noticed.  一位狡猾的律师找到了别人都没注意的漏洞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cunning</td><td>/ˈkʌnɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>狡猾的</td><td>a cunning scheme<br>狡猾的计谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fox was cunning enough to avoid every trap they set.  这只狐狸够狡猾，避开了他们设的每个陷阱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 artful</td><td>/ˈɑːrtfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>技艺高超的；狡猾的</td><td>an artful arrangement<br>巧妙的布置</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chef's artful plating made the dish look like a painting.  厨师巧妙的摆盘让这道菜看起来像幅画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foxy</td><td>/ˈfɑːksi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>狡猾的，机敏的</td><td>a foxy old trader<br>狡猾的老商人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The foxy old trader always knew when to sell everything.  这位狡猾的老商人总知道何时全部卖出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 guileful</del></td><td>/ˈɡaɪlfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>狡诈的，欺骗的</td><td>a guileful negotiator<br>狡诈的谈判者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guileful merchant tricked several villagers out of their savings.  这个狡诈的商人骗走了几位村民的积蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tricky</td><td>/ˈtrɪki/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>狡猾的；棘手的</td><td>a tricky opponent<br>难对付的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parking here is tricky, especially during the busy evening hours.  这里停车很棘手，尤其在傍晚的繁忙时段。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wily</td><td>/ˈwaɪli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>狡诈的，狡猾的</td><td>a wily negotiator<br>狡猾的谈判者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A wily negotiator, he never revealed his lowest acceptable price.  作为狡猾的谈判者，他从不透露自己能接受的最低价。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 artless</td><td>/ˈɑrtləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏技巧的，笨拙的</td><td>an artless imitation<br>拙劣的模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painting was an artless copy of a famous portrait.  这幅画是对一幅名作的拙劣临摹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 guileless</td><td>/ˈɡaɪlləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单纯的，不狡诈的</td><td>a guileless smile<br>天真无邪的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her guileless smile made everyone in the room trust her immediately.  她天真无邪的笑容让屋里所有人立刻信任她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ingenuous</td><td>/ɪnˈdʒenjuəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>天真淳朴的</td><td>an ingenuous young student<br>天真淳朴的年轻学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His ingenuous questions about the city amused the veteran taxi driver greatly.  他关于这座城市的天真提问让老出租车司机大为好笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 innocent</td><td>/ˈɪnəsənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的，无恶意的；无辜的</td><td>an innocent remark<br>无恶意的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What began as an innocent joke ended in a serious quarrel.  一句本无恶意的玩笑最后引发了激烈争吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 undesigning</del></td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈzaɪnɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无心机的，坦率的</td><td>an undesigning honest remark<br>毫无心机的诚实话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His undesigning honesty won friends wherever he happened to travel.  他毫无心机的诚实让他无论走到哪里都能交到朋友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>deft</b></td><td>/deft/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>灵巧的，熟练的</td><td>a deft touch<br>娴熟的手法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 With a few deft strokes the artist finished the small sketch.  画家几笔灵巧的勾画便完成了那幅小素描。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cunning</td><td>/ˈkʌnɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>狡猾的</td><td>a cunning scheme<br>狡猾的计谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fox was cunning enough to avoid every trap they set.  这只狐狸够狡猾，避开了他们设的每个陷阱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adroit</td><td>/əˈdrɔɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟练的，灵巧的</td><td>adroit at negotiation<br>擅长谈判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She proved remarkably adroit at handling hostile questions from reporters.  她在应对记者的敌意提问时表现得非常娴熟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dexterous</td><td>/ˈdekstrəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>动作灵活的；头脑灵活的</td><td>dexterous fingers<br>灵巧的手指</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The surgeon's dexterous hands worked steadily for more than six hours.  这位外科医生灵活的双手稳稳地工作了六个多小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expert</td><td>/ˈekspərt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>专家；熟练的，专业的</td><td>a leading expert<br>顶尖专家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They called in an expert to examine the cracked foundation.  他们请来专家检查开裂的地基。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 masterful</td><td>/ˈmæstərfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>技艺精湛的，娴熟的</td><td>a masterful performance<br>一场技艺精湛的演出</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her masterful handling of the violin silenced the whole hall.  她精湛的小提琴技艺让全场肃静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 virtuoso</td><td>/ˌvɜːrtʃuˈoʊsoʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>技艺大师，演奏名家；技艺精湛的</td><td>a violin virtuoso<br>小提琴演奏大师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The violin virtuoso played the difficult passage without a single mistake.  那位小提琴大师毫无差错地演奏了这段难曲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 awkward</td><td>/ˈɔːkwərd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，不灵巧的；尴尬的</td><td>an awkward silence<br>尴尬的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An awkward silence followed his remark about her divorce.  他谈到她离婚之后，出现了一阵尴尬的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 maladroit</td><td>/ˌmæləˈdrɔɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的</td><td>a maladroit attempt<br>笨拙的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His maladroit attempt at an apology only made the situation worse.  他笨拙的道歉尝试只是让情况变得更糟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ham-handed</td><td>/ˌhæm ˈhændɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨手笨脚的，不圆滑的</td><td>a ham-handed apology<br>笨拙的道歉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His ham-handed attempt to help only made things worse.  他笨拙的帮忙只让事情更糟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amateur</td><td>/ˈæməˌtɜːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>业余爱好者；外行的</td><td>a talented amateur<br>有天赋的业余者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The comet was first spotted by an amateur astronomer.  这颗彗星最先由一位业余天文学家发现。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 artless</td><td>/ˈɑrtləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏技巧的，笨拙的</td><td>an artless imitation<br>拙劣的模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painting was an artless copy of a famous portrait.  这幅画是对一幅名作的拙劣临摹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unprofessional</td><td>/ˌʌnprəˈfeʃənəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不专业的，外行的</td><td>unprofessional conduct<br>不专业的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Arriving late to every meeting looks deeply unprofessional.  每次会议都迟到显得极不专业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unskillful</td><td>/ʌnˈskɪlfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不熟练的，技艺拙劣的</td><td>unskillful workmanship<br>拙劣的手工活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The unskillful repair left the table wobbling badly.  这次拙劣的修理让桌子摇晃得厉害。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>defuse</b></td><td>/ˌdiːˈfjuːz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>化解，缓和（紧张局势）</td><td>defuse the tension<br>缓解紧张气氛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A quiet apology defused the quarrel before it could grow worse.  一句平静的道歉在争吵恶化之前化解了它。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 foment</td><td>/foʊˈment/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>助长，煽动</td><td>foment unrest<br>煽动动乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlets were designed to foment rebellion in the provinces.  这些小册子意在煽动各省叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>defy</b></td><td>/dɪˈfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>蔑视，公然违抗</td><td>defy an order<br>违抗命令</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands of workers defied the ban and gathered in the main square.  数千名工人公然违抗禁令，聚集在中心广场上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mock</td><td>/ˈmɑːk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>假的，模拟的；嘲笑</td><td>a mock trial<br>模拟审判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students held a mock election to learn how voting works.  学生举行模拟选举以了解投票如何运作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rebel</td><td>/rɪˈbel/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>反抗，造反；反叛者</td><td>rebel against authority<br>反抗权威</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students rebelled against the strict new curfew last spring.  学生们去年春天反抗严苛的新宵禁令。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 oppose</td><td>/əˈpoʊz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>反对，抵制</td><td>oppose the new law<br>反对新法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two senators openly opposed the new tax bill.  两位参议员公开反对这项新税案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 confront</td><td>/kənˈfrʌnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>直接对抗，直面</td><td>confront the problem<br>直面问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sooner or later you must confront the problem directly.  你早晚必须直面这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disregard</td><td>/ˌdɪsrɪˈɡɑːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>漠视，不关注；缺乏兴趣，缺乏关心</td><td>total disregard for safety<br>完全不顾安全</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their total disregard for safety rules eventually caused a serious accident.  他们完全不顾安全规定，最终造成了一起严重事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acquiesce</td><td>/ˌækwiˈes/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>勉强同意；默许</td><td>acquiesce in the decision<br>默许该决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She acquiesced in their plan rather than start an argument.  她默许了他们的计划，以免引起争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obey</td><td>/oʊˈbeɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>服从，遵守</td><td>obey the rules<br>遵守规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Drivers must obey the speed limit in school zones.  司机在学校区域必须遵守限速。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 comply with</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪ wɪð/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>遵守，服从</td><td>comply with regulations<br>遵守规章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every factory must comply with the new emission standards.  每家工厂都必须遵守新的排放标准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conform to</td><td>/kənˈfɔrm tuː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>遵守，符合</td><td>conform to the standard<br>符合标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every part must conform to the national safety standard.  每个零件都必须符合国家安全标准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 to</td><td>/ˈtuː/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>介词</td><td>向，到；（构成不定式）</td><td>yield to pressure<br>屈从于压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They refused to yield to pressure from the powerful mining company.  他们拒绝屈从于强大矿业公司的压力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 yield to</td><td>/ˈjiːld tu/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>屈服于，让位于</td><td>yield to temptation<br>经不住诱惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She would not yield to threats of any kind.  她不会屈服于任何形式的威胁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dehydrate</b></td><td>/diːˈhaɪdreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>去除水分，使干燥；使失去活力</td><td>dehydrate fruit for storage<br>把水果脱水储存</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Campers dehydrate fruit and meat so the food lasts for weeks.  露营者把水果和肉脱水，好让食物保存数周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 desiccate</del></td><td>/ˈdesɪkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使干燥，使脱水</td><td>desiccate the soil completely<br>使土壤完全干透</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months without rain desiccated the fields and killed the young corn.  数月无雨使田地干透，幼小的玉米也枯死了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 parch</del></td><td>/pɑːrtʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>烤干，使干透</td><td>parch the dry earth<br>把干土烤裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Weeks without rain had parched the fields into cracked brown dust.  数周无雨已把田地烤得干裂成褐色的尘土。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scorch</td><td>/skɔːrtʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>炙烤，烘干</td><td>scorch the grass brown<br>把草烤成褐色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The blazing sun scorched the grass brown within a single week.  烈日一周之内就把草烤成褐色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sear</td><td>/ˈsɪr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>烤焦，烧灼；使枯萎</td><td>sear the meat quickly<br>快速把肉煎焦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sear the meat quickly on both sides before roasting it.  烤之前先把肉两面快速煎焦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deaden</td><td>/ˈdedən/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使减弱，使麻木</td><td>deaden the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick carpets deaden the sound of footsteps upstairs.  厚地毯减弱了楼上的脚步声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 devitalize</del></td><td>/diːˈvaɪtəlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使失去活力</td><td>devitalize the local economy<br>使当地经济失去活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of neglect devitalized the once thriving downtown district.  多年的忽视使曾经繁荣的市中心失去活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 enervate</del></td><td>/ˈenərveɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>enervate the whole team<br>使全队疲弱无力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long heat wave enervated the whole team before the final match.  漫长的热浪在决赛前使全队疲弱无力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 petrify</td><td>/ˈpetrɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使僵化，使失去活力</td><td>petrify a living tradition<br>使活的传统僵化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Endless rules can petrify a living tradition into empty ritual.  无穷的规矩会使活的传统僵化为空洞的仪式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sap</td><td>/sæp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>削弱，逐渐耗尽</td><td>sap his strength<br>削弱他的力量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Weeks of night shifts sapped the energy of the young doctor completely.  数周的夜班彻底削弱了这位年轻医生的精力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dampen</td><td>/ˈdæmpən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制，压抑（感情、精力等），泼冷水</td><td>dampen his enthusiasm<br>打击他的热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Losing the first game did nothing to dampen the team's spirits.  首场失利丝毫没有打击球队的士气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hydrate</td><td>/ˈhaɪˌdreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>使含水，补水</td><td>hydrate the skin thoroughly<br>给皮肤充分补水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Drink water regularly to hydrate your body during long runs.  长跑时要定时喝水给身体补水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 saturate with water</td><td>/ˈsætʃəreɪt wɪð ˈwɔːtər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使浸透水，饱含水分</td><td>saturate the soil with water<br>使土壤浸透水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy rain saturated the garden soil with water overnight.  大雨一夜之间使花园土壤浸透了水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reconstitute</td><td>/ˌriːˈkɑːnstətuːt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重建，（尤其是通过加水）使复原</td><td>reconstitute dried milk powder<br>把奶粉加水复原</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Simply add warm water to reconstitute the dried milk powder.  只需加温水就能把奶粉复原。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 brace</td><td>/ˈbreɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使振作，鼓励；支撑</td><td>brace yourself for impact<br>做好承受冲击准备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cup of hot tea braced him for the long night.  一杯热茶让他为漫长的夜晚振作起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 energize</td><td>/ˈenərdʒaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使充满活力，激励</td><td>energize the whole team<br>使整个团队振奋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new coach energized a team that had lost hope.  新教练让失去希望的球队重新振奋起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enliven</td><td>/ɪnˈlaɪvn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使活跃，使生动</td><td>enliven a dull party<br>让沉闷的聚会活跃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her jokes enlivened an otherwise dull afternoon meeting.  她的玩笑让本来沉闷的下午会议活跃起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 invigorate</td><td>/ɪnˈvɪɡəreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使精神，使强壮，鼓舞激励</td><td>invigorate the local economy<br>振兴当地经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk in cold air always invigorates my tired mind.  在冷空气中短暂散步总能让我疲惫的头脑重新振作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vitalize</td><td>/ˈvaɪtəlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赋予活力，激励</td><td>vitalize the local economy<br>使当地经济充满活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New investment vitalized the sleepy town within just two years.  新投资在两年内使这个沉寂的小镇焕发活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 vivify</del></td><td>/ˈvɪvɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使生动，赋予生命</td><td>vivify a dull narrative<br>使乏味的叙述生动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vivid dialogue vivifies an otherwise dull historical narrative.  生动的对话使原本乏味的历史叙述活了起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>deify</b></td><td>/ˈdeɪɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>神化，奉若神明</td><td>deify a leader<br>神化领袖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime tried to deify its founder in every textbook.  该政权试图在每本教科书里神化其创始人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aggrandize</td><td>/əˈɡrændaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扩大（权力、财富、声望）</td><td>aggrandize one's power<br>扩张自己的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor used the crisis to aggrandize his own power.  市长利用危机扩大自己的权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 canonize</td><td>/ˈkænənaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使神圣，封为圣徒</td><td>canonize a saint<br>封为圣徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The church voted to canonize the nun a century after her death.  教会在这位修女去世百年后决定将她封为圣徒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dignify</td><td>/ˈdɪɡnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使有尊严，使显庄重</td><td>dignify the ceremony<br>使典礼显得庄重</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her quiet presence dignified the otherwise chaotic opening ceremony.  她安静的在场使原本混乱的开幕式显得庄重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ennoble</td><td>/ɪˈnoʊbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使尊贵，使高尚</td><td>ennoble the spirit<br>使精神高尚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Suffering, he believed, could ennoble an ordinary life.  他相信苦难能使平凡的人生变得高贵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glorify</td><td>/ˈɡlɔːrəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，美化</td><td>glorify war and violence<br>美化战争与暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics said the film glorified war instead of questioning it.  批评者说这部电影美化战争而非质疑它。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 magnify</td><td>/ˈmæɡnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放大；夸大</td><td>magnify the problem<br>夸大问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lens magnifies the specimen four hundred times.  这个镜头把样本放大四百倍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abase</td><td>/əˈbeɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>降低（地位、威望、尊严）</td><td>abase oneself before someone<br>在某人面前自贬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He would not abase himself by begging for the job.  他不愿低声下气地乞求这份工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 degrade</td><td>/dɪˈɡreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，使降级；使退化</td><td>degrade the quality<br>降低质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such insulting jokes degrade everyone who repeats them.  这类侮辱性的笑话贬低了每个转述它们的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 demean</td><td>/dɪˈmiːn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低，降低尊严</td><td>demean oneself<br>自贬身份</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to demean his colleagues in front of clients.  他拒绝在客户面前贬低同事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humble</td><td>/ˈhʌmbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑微的，地位低下的；谦逊的</td><td>humble origins<br>卑微的出身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rose from humble origins to lead the company.  他出身卑微，最终领导了这家公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humiliate</td><td>/hjuːˈmɪlieɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>羞辱，使丧失尊严</td><td>humiliate him in public<br>当众羞辱他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher never humiliated a student for asking a simple question.  这位老师从不因学生问简单的问题而羞辱他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 debasement</td><td>/dəˈbeɪsmənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>贬低，降格</td><td>the debasement of language<br>语言的降格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lamented the steady debasement of public political language.  他哀叹公共政治语言的持续降格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 7

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>deject</b></del></td><td>/dɪˈdʒekt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使沮丧</td><td>deject the whole team<br>使全队沮丧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing dejects a coach more than seeing his players quit trying.  没有什么比看到球员放弃努力更让教练沮丧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 oppress</td><td>/əˈpres/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使压抑沮丧；压迫</td><td>oppress the spirit<br>使人精神压抑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gloomy weather oppressed everyone stuck inside the small cabin.  阴沉的天气让困在小屋里的每个人都心情压抑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sadden</td><td>/ˈsædən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使悲伤</td><td>sadden her deeply<br>使她深感悲伤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The news of his death saddened the entire small village.  他去世的消息让整个小村庄都感到悲伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weigh down</td><td>/weɪ ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使心情沉重，压垮</td><td>weigh down with worry<br>因忧虑而心情沉重</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Debt and illness weighed him down for many difficult years.  债务和疾病在许多艰难岁月里压得他喘不过气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bum out</td><td>/bʌm ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使扫兴，使沮丧</td><td>really bum me out<br>让我很扫兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cancelled concert really bummed out all of us.  演唱会取消让我们所有人都很扫兴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 brighten</td><td>/ˈbraɪtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使愉快开朗；使明亮</td><td>brighten her mood<br>让她心情变好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short phone call from home brightened her gloomy mood.  一个来自家里的短电话让她阴郁的心情变好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 buoy</td><td>/ˈbuːi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使充满勇气和力量，使振作</td><td>buoy his spirits<br>使他振作起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Kind letters from strangers buoyed her spirits during the long illness.  陌生人的暖心来信在她久病期间让她振作起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lighten</td><td>/ˈlaɪtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使轻松愉快；减轻</td><td>lighten the mood<br>缓和气氛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A silly joke lightened the mood in the tense room.  一个傻笑话缓和了紧张房间里的气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rejoice</td><td>/rɪˈdʒɔɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>欣喜，喜悦</td><td>rejoice at the news<br>为消息而欣喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village rejoiced at the news of the safe return.  听到平安归来的消息，全村都欣喜不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cheer up</td><td>/tʃɪr ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使高兴起来</td><td>cheer up a sad friend<br>让难过的朋友开心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We took her to the beach to cheer her up.  我们带她去海边让她开心起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>deleterious</b></td><td>/ˌdeləˈtɪriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的</td><td>deleterious effects on health<br>对健康的有害影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Long night shifts had a deleterious effect on the nurses' health.  长期夜班对护士们的健康产生了有害影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adverse</td><td>/ædˈvɜːrs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，不利的</td><td>adverse side effects<br>不良副作用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The drug had adverse effects on several elderly patients.  这种药对几位老年病人产生了不良影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 baleful</td><td>/ˈbeɪlfl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的</td><td>a baleful influence<br>有害的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His baleful influence on the young players worried the coach.  他对年轻球员的有害影响让教练担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 baneful</td><td>/ˈbeɪnfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，有毒的</td><td>a baneful habit<br>一个有害的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Gossip had a baneful effect on the whole department.  流言对整个部门产生了有害的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 detrimental</td><td>/ˌdetrəˈmentəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，不利的</td><td>detrimental to your health<br>对你的健康有害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too much screen time is detrimental to a child's eyesight.  过多看屏幕对孩子的视力有害。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mischievous</td><td>/ˈmɪstʃɪvəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的；调皮的</td><td>a mischievous rumor<br>有害的谣言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mischievous rumor damaged her reputation for several years.  这个有害的谣言损害了她多年的名誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 nocuous</del></td><td>/ˈnɑːkjuəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，有毒的</td><td>nocuous fumes<br>有害的烟气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Factory chimneys released nocuous gases into the evening air.  工厂烟囱把有害气体排入夜晚的空气中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 noxious</td><td>/ˈnɑːkʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的，有毒的；产生强烈厌恶的</td><td>noxious fumes<br>有毒的烟气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Noxious fumes from the factory forced the school to close for two days.  工厂排出的有毒烟气迫使学校停课两天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pernicious</td><td>/pərˈnɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的</td><td>a pernicious rumor<br>极有害的谣言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A pernicious rumor about the school spread quickly through the town.  一则关于学校的极有害谣言迅速在镇上传开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 beneficial</td><td>/ˌbenəˈfɪʃəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益的，有利的</td><td>beneficial to your health<br>对健康有益</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular walking is beneficial to both your heart and mood.  经常步行对心脏和情绪都有益。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 salutary</td><td>/ˈsæljəteri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益健康的；有利的，利好的</td><td>a salutary lesson<br>有益的教训</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The failed launch had a salutary effect on the careless habits of the team.  那次发射失败对团队的马虎习惯起了有益的作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 salubrious</td><td>/səˈluːbriəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益健康的</td><td>a salubrious mountain climate<br>有益健康的山地气候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors sent him to a salubrious mountain village to recover his strength.  医生把他送到有益健康的山村去恢复体力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wholesome</td><td>/ˈhoʊlsəm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益身心健康的</td><td>wholesome home-cooked food<br>有益健康的家常饭菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children grew strong on wholesome food and plenty of fresh air.  孩子们靠有益健康的食物和充足的新鲜空气长得很结实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 benign</td><td>/bɪˈnaɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的</td><td>a benign influence<br>有益无害的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The change in policy proved benign for most small farmers.  事实证明，政策变化对大多数小农户是无害的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 harmless</td><td>/ˈhɑːrmləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的，不会造成伤害的</td><td>a harmless little joke<br>无伤大雅的玩笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The snake looks frightening but is completely harmless to humans.  这条蛇看起来可怕，但对人完全无害。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 innocuous</td><td>/ɪˈnɑːkjuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的；不会引起敌意的</td><td>an innocuous question<br>一个无恶意的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What seemed an innocuous question turned out to reveal her deepest secret.  看似无恶意的问题最终却揭露了她最深的秘密。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>deliberate</b></td><td>/dɪˈlɪbərət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深思熟虑的，故意的</td><td>a deliberate attempt<br>故意的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delay was deliberate, designed to weaken our position.  这次延误是故意的，目的是削弱我们的立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 calculated</td><td>/ˈkælkjuleɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>经过深思熟虑的，有意的</td><td>a calculated risk<br>经过权衡的风险</td></tr>
