<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investing now was a calculated risk that finally paid off.  现在投资是经过盘算的风险，最终有了回报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 considered</td><td>/kənˈsɪdərd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>经过深思的，慎重的</td><td>a considered opinion<br>深思后的意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That is my considered judgment after reading the whole report.  这是我读完整份报告后的慎重判断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reasoned</td><td>/ˈriːzənd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>经过推理的，理由充分的</td><td>a reasoned argument<br>有理有据的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She presented a reasoned case for keeping the library open.  她提出了有理有据的理由要求图书馆继续开放。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thoughtful</td><td>/ˈθɔːtfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>深思熟虑的；体贴的</td><td>a thoughtful measured reply<br>深思熟虑的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave a thoughtful reply after considering the question carefully.  她仔细考虑问题后给出深思熟虑的回答。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impetuous</td><td>/ɪmˈpetʃuəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冲动的，性急的，轻率的</td><td>an impetuous decision<br>轻率的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One impetuous remark at the meeting cost him a valuable client.  会上一句冲动的话让他失去了一位重要客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 casual</td><td>/ˈkæʒəwəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>随意的，非故意的</td><td>a casual remark<br>随口的一句话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A casual remark at dinner accidentally revealed the whole secret.  晚餐时随口一句话意外揭出了整个秘密。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unadvised</del></td><td>/ˌʌnədˈvaɪzd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻率的，未经考虑的</td><td>an unadvised hasty action<br>轻率仓促的行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unadvised comment to reporters caused weeks of serious trouble.  他对记者轻率的评论造成了数周的严重麻烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 uncalculated</del></td><td>/ʌnˈkælkjəleɪtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未经算计的，无心的</td><td>an uncalculated act of kindness<br>无心的善举</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her uncalculated kindness to strangers impressed everyone who watched.  她对陌生人不加算计的善意让旁观者都很感动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unconsidered</td><td>/ˌʌnkənˈsɪdərd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未经考虑的，草率的</td><td>an unconsidered reply<br>未经考虑的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An unconsidered reply in the interview cost him the job.  面试中一句未经考虑的回答让他丢了工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unstudied</td><td>/ʌnˈstʌdid/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自然的，不做作的</td><td>unstudied natural charm<br>天然不做作的魅力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unstudied charm won over the entire skeptical audience.  她自然不做作的魅力赢得了全场持疑观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>delicacy</b></td><td>/ˈdelɪkəsi/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（外貌、结构等）精致</td><td>the delicacy of the lace<br>蕾丝的精致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone admired the delicacy of the carving on the ivory box.  大家都赞叹象牙盒上雕刻的精致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 daintiness</del></td><td>/ˈdeɪntinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>精致，秀气</td><td>the daintiness of the teacups<br>茶杯的精致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The daintiness of the porcelain teacups delighted every visiting guest.  瓷茶杯的精致让每位来客都很喜欢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 crudity</td><td>/ˈkrudɪti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>粗鲁，粗俗；粗糙</td><td>the crudity of his jokes<br>他玩笑的粗俗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crudity of his remarks offended the guests at dinner.  他言辞的粗俗令晚宴上的客人不快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 coarseness</td><td>/ˈkɔːrsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>粗糙；粗俗</td><td>the coarseness of the fabric<br>布料的粗糙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coarseness of the cheap fabric irritated her sensitive skin.  廉价布料的粗糙刺激了她敏感的皮肤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 roughness</td><td>/ˈrʌfnəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>粗糙；粗暴，粗鲁</td><td>the roughness of his manner<br>他举止的粗鲁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The roughness of his reply startled everyone at the table.  他回答的粗鲁让满桌人都吃了一惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>delirium</b></td><td>/dɪˈlɪriəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>精神错乱；极度兴奋，发狂</td><td>in a feverish delirium<br>处于发烧的昏乱中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 For two nights the fever kept him in a restless delirium.  高烧使他两夜都处在不安的精神错乱之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agitation</td><td>/ˌædʒəˈteɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>焦虑不安，激动</td><td>in a state of agitation<br>处于焦躁不安状态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He paced the hallway in a state of visible agitation.  他在走廊里踱步，显得焦躁不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distraction</td><td>/dɪsˈtrækʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>心烦意乱，精神错乱</td><td>drive him to distraction<br>使他心烦意乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The constant noise drove the exhausted writer to distraction.  持续的噪音让疲惫的作家心烦意乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hysteria</td><td>/hɪˈsteriə/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>歇斯底里，狂热</td><td>mass hysteria in the crowd<br>人群中的集体狂热</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rumors of invasion produced mass hysteria across the small town.  入侵的谣言在小镇引发集体歇斯底里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rage</td><td>/reɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>暴怒</td><td>fly into a rage<br>勃然大怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He flew into a rage when the waiter spilled the soup.  服务员把汤打翻时，他勃然大怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rampage</td><td>/ˈræmˌpeɪdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>横冲直撞，暴怒乱闯</td><td>go on a rampage<br>大肆撒野闹事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Elephants rampaged through the village, destroying several huts.  大象横冲直撞穿过村子，毁了几间棚屋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uproar</td><td>/ˈʌˌprɔːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>喧嚣，骚动</td><td>cause an uproar<br>引起轩然大波</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new tax plan caused an uproar among small business owners.  新税收方案在小企业主中引起轩然大波。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>delusion</b></td><td>/dɪˈluːʒn/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>错觉，妄想</td><td>labor under a delusion<br>抱着错误的幻想</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He clung to the delusion that the company would soon rehire him.  他固执地抱着公司很快会重新雇用他的妄想。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hallucination</td><td>/həˌluːsɪˈneɪʃn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>幻觉</td><td>suffer hallucinations<br>出现幻觉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The high fever gave him vivid hallucinations that lasted through the night.  高烧让他产生了持续整夜的鲜明幻觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 illusion</td><td>/ɪˈluːʒən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>错觉，幻想</td><td>an optical illusion<br>视觉错觉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mirrors create the illusion that the room is twice as large.  镜子造成了房间大了一倍的错觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unreality</td><td>/ˌʌnriˈæləti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>虚幻，不真实</td><td>a sense of unreality<br>虚幻不真实的感觉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A strange sense of unreality followed the accident for days.  事故之后有好几天都伴随着奇怪的不真实感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 falsehood</td><td>/ˈfɔːlshʊd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>谎言</td><td>spread a deliberate falsehood<br>散布故意的谎言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He repeated the falsehood so often that people believed it.  他把这个谎言说了太多次，人们竟然信了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 misconception</td><td>/ˌmɪskənˈsepʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>误解，错误观念</td><td>a common misconception<br>常见的误解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is a common misconception that bats are completely blind.  蝙蝠完全失明是个常见的误解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 truth</td><td>/ˈtruːθ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>真相，真实</td><td>tell the truth<br>说出真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took ten years for the truth about the accident to emerge.  这场事故的真相花了十年才浮出水面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 verity</td><td>/ˈverəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>真理，真实</td><td>an eternal verity<br>永恒的真理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The play explores eternal verities about love and human loss.  这部戏探讨关于爱与失去的永恒真理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 transparent</td><td>/trænsˈpærənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有歧义的，清晰易懂的</td><td>a transparent explanation<br>清晰易懂的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a transparent explanation that even beginners understood immediately.  他给出清晰易懂的解释，连初学者都立刻明白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>deluge</b></td><td>/ˈdeljuːdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大暴雨；大量</td><td>a deluge of complaints<br>大量投诉涌来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The announcement brought a deluge of angry letters to the editor.  这项公告引来大量愤怒的读者来信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cloudburst</td><td>/ˈklaʊdˌbɜːrst/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>暴雨，倾盆大雨</td><td>a sudden afternoon cloudburst<br>午后突降的暴雨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sudden cloudburst flooded the narrow streets within ten minutes.  一场突降暴雨十分钟内就淹了狭窄街道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 downfall</td><td>/ˈdaʊnˌfɔːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>垮台，衰落</td><td>lead to his downfall<br>导致他垮台</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Greed eventually led to the powerful banker's sudden downfall.  贪婪最终导致这位有权势的银行家突然垮台。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 downpour</td><td>/ˈdaʊnpɔr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>倾盆大雨</td><td>a heavy downpour<br>一场大雨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A heavy downpour soaked the crowd waiting outside the stadium.  一场大雨把在体育场外等候的人群淋透了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rainstorm</td><td>/ˈreɪnˌstɔːrm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>暴风雨</td><td>a violent rainstorm<br>猛烈的暴风雨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A violent rainstorm knocked out power across the whole valley.  一场猛烈暴风雨使整个山谷停电。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inundation</td><td>/ˌɪnənˈdeɪʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>洪水，淹没；大量</td><td>the inundation of farmland<br>农田被淹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dam failure caused the inundation of hundreds of farms.  水坝失事导致数百个农场被淹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overflow</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈfloʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>溢出，泛滥</td><td>overflow the river banks<br>河水漫过堤岸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Melting snow made the river overflow its banks last spring.  去年春天融雪使河水漫过堤岸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spate</td><td>/speɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大量</td><td>a spate of complaints<br>大量投诉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The store received a spate of complaints about the faulty heaters.  商店收到大量关于取暖器故障的投诉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 torrent</td><td>/ˈtɔːrənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>激流；大量</td><td>a torrent of muddy water<br>一股浑浊的激流</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A torrent of muddy water swept the wooden bridge away.  一股浑浊激流把木桥冲走了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 drizzle</td><td>/ˈdrɪzəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>细雨，小雨</td><td>a light morning drizzle<br>清晨的小细雨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A light drizzle fell all morning but nobody bothered complaining.  整个上午下着小雨，但没人抱怨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>delve</b></td><td>/delv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>探究，钻研</td><td>delve into ancient records<br>钻研古代记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She delved into the family archives and found a startling old letter.  她钻研家族档案，发现了一封令人震惊的旧信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 probe</td><td>/proʊb/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>深入调查</td><td>probe deeply into the scandal<br>深入调查这桩丑闻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investigators continued to probe deeply into the company's missing funds.  调查人员继续深入调查公司资金失踪的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 investigate</td><td>/ɪnˈvestɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>调查，研究</td><td>investigate the cause thoroughly<br>彻底调查原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police are investigating the cause of the warehouse fire.  警方正在调查仓库火灾的原因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inquire into</td><td>/ɪnˈkwaɪr ˈɪntuː/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>调查，探询</td><td>inquire into the matter<br>调查此事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A committee will inquire into the missing department funds.  一个委员会将调查该部门丢失的资金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 look into</td><td>/lʊk ˈɪntuː/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>调查，了解</td><td>look into the complaint<br>调查这项投诉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager promised to look into the customer's angry complaint.  经理承诺会调查这位顾客的愤怒投诉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dig into</td><td>/dɪɡ ˈɪntuː/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>深挖，深入探究</td><td>dig into the archives<br>深挖档案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reporters dug into the archives and found the missing letters.  记者深挖档案，找到了失踪的信件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>demagogue</b></td><td>/ˈdeməɡɑːɡ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>蛊惑民心的政客</td><td>a dangerous populist demagogue<br>危险的民粹政客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The demagogue blamed foreigners for every problem the province faced.  那个蛊惑民心的政客把全省的每个问题都归咎于外国人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 firebrand</td><td>/ˈfaɪərˌbrænd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>煽动者，鼓动分子</td><td>a young political firebrand<br>年轻的政治煽动者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young firebrand stirred the crowd with furious speeches.  这个年轻的煽动者用愤怒的演讲激起人群。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 fomenter</del></td><td>/foʊˈmɛntər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>煽动者，挑起者</td><td>a fomenter of unrest<br>动乱的煽动者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials called him a fomenter of unrest in the province.  官员称他是该省动乱的煽动者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incendiary</td><td>/ɪnˈsendieri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>煽动者；纵火者</td><td>a political incendiary<br>政治煽动者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government labeled the anonymous pamphleteer a dangerous incendiary.  政府把这位匿名小册子作者称为危险的煽动者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 instigator</td><td>/ˈɪnstəˌɡeɪtər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>煽动者，主使者</td><td>the instigator of the riot<br>骚乱的主使者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police identified the instigator of the riot from video footage.  警方从录像中认出了骚乱的主使者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 kindler</td><td>/ˈkɪndlər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>点燃者，煽动者</td><td>a kindler of rebellion<br>叛乱的点火者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 History remembers him as a kindler of popular rebellion.  历史记住他是民众叛乱的点火者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provocateur</td><td>/prəˌvɑːkəˈtɜːr/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>挑动者，煽动分子</td><td>a political provocateur<br>政治挑动者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The blogger acted as a provocateur during the tense election.  这位博主在紧张的选举期间充当挑动者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>demanding</b></td><td>/dɪˈmændɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难取悦的，难满足的；费时间花心思的</td><td>a demanding new job<br>费心费力的新工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Caring for three small children is demanding work with no holidays.  照顾三个小孩是费时间花心思且没有休假的工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exacting</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæktɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严格的，苛求的；费时间花心思的</td><td>exacting quality standards<br>严格的质量标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The airline maintains exacting standards for every part it installs.  这家航空公司对安装的每个部件都保持严格的标准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fastidious</td><td>/fæˈstɪdiəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>挑剔的，极仔细的，追求完美的</td><td>fastidious about details<br>对细节极挑剔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is fastidious about details and rereads every line twice.  她对细节极为挑剔，每一行都要读两遍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 finical</td><td>/ˈfɪnɪkəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过分挑剔的</td><td>a finical eater<br>挑食的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their finical guest rejected almost every dish on the table.  他们挑剔的客人几乎拒绝了桌上每道菜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 finicking</del></td><td>/ˈfɪnɪkɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>挑剔的，过分讲究的</td><td>finicking attention to detail<br>对细节过分讲究</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His finicking attention to detail slowed the whole project down.  他对细节的过分讲究拖慢了整个项目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fussy</td><td>/ˈfʌsi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>挑剔的，过分讲究的</td><td>fussy about small details<br>对小细节很挑剔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old tailor is fussy about the smallest details.  这位老裁缝对最小的细节都很挑剔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 picky</td><td>/ˈpɪki/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>挑剔的，难满足的</td><td>a picky eater<br>挑食的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My nephew is a picky eater who refuses all vegetables.  我侄子很挑食，什么蔬菜都不吃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arduous</td><td>/ˈɑːrdʒuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以做到的，费劲的</td><td>an arduous journey<br>艰苦的旅程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The climb proved far more arduous than any of them expected.  这次攀登远比他们任何人预想的都费劲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 burdensome</td><td>/ˈbɜːrdənsəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>繁重的，累人的</td><td>burdensome regulations<br>繁琐的规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new paperwork proved burdensome for small rural clinics.  新的文书工作对乡村小诊所来说很繁重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 challenging</td><td>/ˈtʃæləndʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有挑战性的，费心力的</td><td>a challenging assignment<br>有挑战性的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor gave us a challenging assignment due next week.  教授给了我们一个下周交的有挑战性作业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grueling</td><td>/ˈɡruːəlɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>累人的，折磨人的</td><td>a grueling schedule<br>折磨人的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team endured a grueling schedule of twelve games in three weeks.  球队熬过了三周十二场比赛这种折磨人的赛程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 killing</td><td>/ˈkɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>极累人的，要命的</td><td>a killing work schedule<br>要命的工作日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They kept a killing schedule of twelve shows every week.  他们保持着每周十二场演出的要命日程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laborious</td><td>/ləˈbɔːriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>费力的，艰苦的；勤奋的</td><td>a laborious process<br>费力的过程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Copying the manuscript by hand proved a slow and laborious process.  手抄这份手稿是一个缓慢而费力的过程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 onerous</td><td>/ˈɑːnərəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>繁重的，费力的</td><td>onerous responsibilities<br>繁重的责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Caring for the archive proved more onerous than the volunteers had expected.  照管这份档案比志愿者们预想的更费时费心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 persnickety</td><td>/pərˈsnɪkəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过分挑剔的</td><td>a persnickety inspector<br>过分挑剔的检查员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The persnickety inspector rejected the wall over one small crack.  这位挑剔的检查员因一条小裂缝就否决了那面墙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 taxing</td><td>/ˈtæksɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>繁重的，费力的</td><td>a taxing task<br>繁重的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Caring for two infants proved a taxing task for the young couple.  照顾两个婴儿对这对年轻夫妻是繁重的任务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 light</td><td>/ˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻微的，不严重的</td><td>a light meal<br>一顿便餐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He suffered only light injuries in the accident.  他在事故中只受了轻伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unchallenging</td><td>/ˌʌnˈtʃælɪndʒɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有挑战性的，轻松的</td><td>an unchallenging job<br>没有挑战的工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He quit the unchallenging position after only three months.  他只干了三个月就辞掉那份没有挑战性的工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undemanding</td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈmændɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>要求不高的，轻松的</td><td>an undemanding course<br>要求不高的课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She chose an undemanding role so she could rest more.  她选了个轻松的角色以便多休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 8

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>demolition</b></td><td>/ˌdeməˈlɪʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>拆除，拆毁；破坏</td><td>demolition of the old building<br>拆除那座旧建筑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Residents protested the demolition of the last old theater downtown.  居民抗议拆毁市中心最后一座老剧院。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 annihilation</td><td>/əˌnaɪəˈleɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>彻底毁灭，歼灭</td><td>the annihilation of an army<br>一支军队被歼灭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The battle ended in the near annihilation of the invading army.  战役以入侵军队几近被歼灭而结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decimation</td><td>/ˌdesəˈmeɪʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大量毁灭，大批死亡</td><td>the decimation of wildlife<br>野生动物大量死亡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Logging caused the decimation of wildlife in that ancient forest.  伐木导致那片古老森林野生动物大量死亡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 devastation</td><td>/ˌdevəˈsteɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>毁坏，破坏</td><td>widespread devastation after the storm<br>风暴后的大范围毁坏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hurricane left widespread devastation along the entire coastline.  飓风在整条海岸线留下大范围毁坏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 havoc</td><td>/ˈhævək/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大混乱</td><td>wreak havoc on crops<br>对庄稼造成大破坏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The flood wreaked havoc on roads and bridges throughout the valley.  洪水对整个山谷的道路和桥梁造成了大破坏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wreckage</td><td>/ˈrekədʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>残骸，破坏后的残余</td><td>sift through the wreckage<br>在残骸中翻找</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investigators sifted through the wreckage looking for the flight recorder.  调查人员在残骸中翻找飞行记录仪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 building</td><td>/ˈbɪldɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>建造；建筑物</td><td>the building of a bridge<br>桥梁的建造</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The building of the new bridge took almost four years.  新桥的建造花了将近四年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 construction</td><td>/kənˈstrʌkʃən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>建造，施工</td><td>under construction<br>在建造中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library has been under construction since early last spring.  图书馆自去年初春以来一直在建。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 erection</td><td>/ɪˈrekʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>建立，竖立</td><td>the erection of a monument<br>纪念碑的竖立</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The erection of the monument took the workers six months.  纪念碑的竖立花了工人六个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 raising</td><td>/ˈreɪzɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>建造，竖起；抚养</td><td>the raising of a barn<br>谷仓的搭建</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors gathered for the raising of the new wooden barn.  邻居们聚在一起搭建新的木谷仓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>demonstrate</b></td><td>/ˈdemənstreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（通过证据）证明，表明</td><td>demonstrate the theory clearly<br>清楚地证明该理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The experiment demonstrated the theory clearly to every skeptical observer.  实验向每位持疑的观察者清楚证明了该理论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exemplify</td><td>/ɪɡˈzempləˌfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>例证，是…的典范</td><td>exemplify the new style<br>例证这种新风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This painting exemplifies the new style better than any other.  这幅画比其他任何作品都更能例证这种新风格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 instance</td><td>/ˈɪnstəns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>举例说明；例子</td><td>cite a specific instance<br>举出具体例子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She cited a specific instance to support her broad claim.  她举出具体例子支持自己的宏观论断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unable to prove</td><td>/ʌnˈeɪbəl tuː ˈpruːv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无法证明</td><td>unable to prove the claim<br>无法证明该说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Prosecutors were unable to prove the claim beyond reasonable doubt.  检方无法在合理怀疑之外证明该指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>demoralize</b></td><td>/dɪˈmɔːrəlaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使士气低落；贬低，使堕落</td><td>demoralize the troops<br>使部队士气低落</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repeated pay cuts demoralized the staff more than the long hours.  反复减薪比长时间工作更让员工士气低落。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 daunt</td><td>/dɔːnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使胆怯，吓倒</td><td>daunt the bravest soldiers<br>吓倒最勇敢的士兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The steep climb ahead did not daunt the experienced mountain guide.  前方陡峭的攀登丝毫没有吓倒这位经验丰富的山地导游。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dishearten</td><td>/dɪsˈhɑːrtn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使沮丧，使失去信心</td><td>disheartened by the news<br>因这消息而沮丧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The string of early losses disheartened even the most loyal fans.  开局连输让最忠实的球迷也失去了信心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dismay</td><td>/dɪsˈmeɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使失去勇气；使不安，使焦虑</td><td>dismay his supporters<br>使支持者沮丧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The abrupt policy reversal dismayed his supporters and delighted his rivals.  政策的突然反转让他的支持者沮丧，让对手高兴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 dispirit</del></td><td>/dɪˈspɪrɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使沮丧，使气馁</td><td>dispirit the whole team<br>使全队士气低落</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The endless rain dispirited even the most cheerful campers.  连绵的阴雨让最开朗的营员也沮丧起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unnerve</td><td>/ʌnˈnɜːrv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使紧张不安，使气馁</td><td>unnerve the young witness<br>使年轻证人紧张不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden silence in the courtroom unnerved the young witness completely.  法庭上突然的寂静让年轻证人完全紧张起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abase</td><td>/əˈbeɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>降低（地位、威望、尊严）</td><td>abase oneself before someone<br>在某人面前自贬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He would not abase himself by begging for the job.  他不愿低声下气地乞求这份工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 corrupt</td><td>/kəˈrʌpt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>腐败的；使腐化，收买</td><td>a corrupt official<br>一名腐败官员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The corrupt inspector accepted cash to ignore the violations.  这名腐败的检查员收现金来无视违规。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debauch</td><td>/dɪˈbɔtʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使堕落，腐化</td><td>debauch the young<br>腐化年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics claimed the film would debauch impressionable young viewers.  批评者称这部电影会腐蚀易受影响的年轻观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 degrade</td><td>/dɪˈɡreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，使降级；使退化</td><td>degrade the quality<br>降低质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such insulting jokes degrade everyone who repeats them.  这类侮辱性的笑话贬低了每个转述它们的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demean</td><td>/dɪˈmiːn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低，降低尊严</td><td>demean oneself<br>自贬身份</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to demean his colleagues in front of clients.  他拒绝在客户面前贬低同事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 invigorate</td><td>/ɪnˈvɪɡəreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使精神，使强壮，鼓舞激励</td><td>invigorate the local economy<br>振兴当地经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk in cold air always invigorates my tired mind.  在冷空气中短暂散步总能让我疲惫的头脑重新振作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cheer</td><td>/ˈtʃɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>欢呼，喝彩；使高兴</td><td>a loud cheer<br>一阵响亮的欢呼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A loud cheer rose from the crowd when the goal was scored.  进球时人群中响起一阵响亮的欢呼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 elevate</td><td>/ˈelɪveɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提升，提高；使兴奋</td><td>elevate the mind<br>提升心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good literature can elevate the mind and refine one's taste.  优秀的文学能提升心智、陶冶品味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ennoble</td><td>/ɪˈnoʊbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使尊贵，使高尚</td><td>ennoble the spirit<br>使精神高尚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Suffering, he believed, could ennoble an ordinary life.  他相信苦难能使平凡的人生变得高贵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uplift</td><td>/ʌpˈlɪft/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>振奋，鼓舞；提高</td><td>uplift the spirits<br>振奋精神</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her cheerful letters uplifted him during the long illness.  她愉快的信在他久病期间给了他鼓舞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>demotic</b></td><td>/dɪˈmɑːtɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>通俗的，大众化的</td><td>demotic speech and slang<br>通俗口语和俚语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novelist wrote in demotic language that ordinary readers loved.  这位小说家用普通读者喜爱的通俗语言写作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 profound</td><td>/proʊˈfaʊnd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深刻的，深远的</td><td>a profound influence<br>深远的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her death had a profound effect on the whole family.  她的死对整个家庭产生了深远的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>demur</b></td><td>/dɪˈmɜːr/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>表示异议，反对</td><td>demur at the suggestion<br>对建议表示异议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She demurred at the suggestion that she should resign.  对于让她辞职的建议，她表示了异议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 challenge</td><td>/ˈtʃæləndʒ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>质疑，对…提出挑战</td><td>challenge the theory<br>质疑该理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several scientists challenged the conclusions of the new study.  几位科学家质疑这项新研究的结论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exception</td><td>/ɪkˈsepʃən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>反对，异议；例外</td><td>take exception to a remark<br>对某话表示反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took exception to her remark about his careless research.  他对她关于他研究草率的话表示反对。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 expostulation</del></td><td>/ɪkˌspɑːstʃəˈleɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>抗议，规劝</td><td>ignore his angry expostulation<br>无视他愤怒的抗议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee ignored his angry expostulation and approved the plan.  委员会无视他愤怒的抗议，通过了计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fuss</td><td>/fʌs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>小题大做，抱怨；忙乱</td><td>make a fuss about<br>为…大惊小怪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't make a fuss about a tiny stain on the tablecloth.  别为桌布上一小块污渍小题大做。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 kick</td><td>/ˈkɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>反对，抱怨；踢</td><td>kick about the new rules<br>对新规定表示不满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Employees kicked about the new rules at every staff meeting.  员工在每次员工会上都对新规定表示不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 protest</td><td>/ˈproʊˌtest/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>抗议，反对</td><td>register a formal protest<br>提出正式抗议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ambassador registered a formal protest with the foreign ministry.  大使向外交部提出正式抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remonstrance</td><td>/rɪˈmɑːnstrəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>抗议，规劝</td><td>a formal written remonstrance<br>正式的书面抗议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers sent a written remonstrance to the district governor.  村民向区长递交了一份书面抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stink</td><td>/ˈstɪŋk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>大声抗议，抱怨；臭味</td><td>raise a stink about it<br>为此大吵大闹抗议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parents raised a stink about the sudden tuition increase.  家长们为学费突然上涨大闹抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accept</td><td>/əkˈsept/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>接受，同意，承认</td><td>accept responsibility<br>承担责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She accepted the award on behalf of her entire research team.  她代表整个研究团队接受了这个奖项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accede</td><td>/əkˈsiːd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>同意，答应；就任</td><td>accede to a request<br>答应请求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government finally acceded to the union's demands.  政府最终同意了工会的要求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>denigrate</b></td><td>/ˈdenɪɡreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诋毁，污蔑</td><td>denigrate a rival<br>诋毁对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He never denigrates his opponents, even after losing badly.  即使惨败，他也从不诋毁对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 belittle</td><td>/bɪˈlɪtəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低，轻视</td><td>belittle her achievements<br>贬低她的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't belittle her achievements just because she works quietly.  别因为她默默工作就贬低她的成就。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 depreciate</td><td>/dɪˈpriːʃieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低…的价值</td><td>depreciate his contribution<br>贬低他的贡献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not depreciate the value of patience in such long negotiations.  不要贬低耐心在这样漫长谈判中的价值。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 derogate</td><td>/ˈderəɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>贬损，诋毁</td><td>derogate from an achievement<br>贬损某项成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing should derogate from her remarkable achievement here.  什么都不应贬损她在这里的非凡成就。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dismiss</td><td>/dɪˈsmɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解散，打发走；解雇；不予考虑</td><td>dismiss the class<br>宣布下课</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher dismissed the class ten minutes ahead of time.  老师提前十分钟宣布下课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disparage</td><td>/dɪˈspærɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低；轻蔑地说</td><td>disparage someone's efforts<br>贬低某人的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not disparage her work simply because she is young.  不要仅仅因为她年轻就贬低她的工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 honor</td><td>/ˈɑːnər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>尊敬，给以荣誉；荣誉</td><td>honor the veterans<br>表彰老兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city honored the veterans with a quiet morning ceremony.  该市用一场安静的晨间仪式表彰老兵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acclaim</td><td>/əˈkleɪm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>称赞</td><td>win critical acclaim<br>赢得评论界赞誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won wide critical acclaim across Europe and Asia.  她的第一部小说在欧洲和亚洲赢得了广泛的评论界赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 applaud</td><td>/əˈplɔːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>称赞，赞成；鼓掌</td><td>applaud the decision<br>赞成这一决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Teachers applauded the decision to reduce homework for young pupils.  老师们赞成给低年级学生减少作业的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exalt</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːlt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞扬；提升（地位）</td><td>exalt to the skies<br>推崇到极点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics exalt her early novels as masterpieces of the era.  评论家将她早期的小说赞为那个时代的杰作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extol</td><td>/ɪkˈstoʊl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美；吹捧</td><td>extol his virtues<br>赞美他的品德</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors still extol his kindness during the long winter flood.  邻居们至今仍赞美他在漫长冬季洪水期间的善举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glorify</td><td>/ˈɡlɔːrəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，美化</td><td>glorify war and violence<br>美化战争与暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics said the film glorified war instead of questioning it.  批评者说这部电影美化战争而非质疑它。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 magnify</td><td>/ˈmæɡnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放大；夸大</td><td>magnify the problem<br>夸大问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lens magnifies the specimen four hundred times.  这个镜头把样本放大四百倍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>disingenuous</b></td><td>/ˌdɪsɪnˈdʒenjuəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不诚恳的，假装无知的</td><td>a disingenuous argument<br>不诚恳的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is disingenuous to claim you knew nothing about it.  声称你对此毫不知情是不诚恳的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insincere</td><td>/ˌɪnsɪnˈsɪr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不诚实的，虚伪的</td><td>an insincere apology<br>虚伪的道歉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His insincere apology only made the situation considerably worse.  他虚伪的道歉只让情况更糟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 affected</td><td>/əˈfektɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>装出来的，做作的</td><td>an affected accent<br>装出来的口音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke with an affected accent that fooled nobody present.  她用装出来的口音说话，没骗过在场任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 artful</td><td>/ˈɑːrtfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>技艺高超的；狡猾的</td><td>an artful arrangement<br>巧妙的布置</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chef's artful plating made the dish look like a painting.  厨师巧妙的摆盘让这道菜看起来像幅画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dishonest</td><td>/dɪˈsɑːnəst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不诚实的，欺骗的</td><td>a dishonest business practice<br>不诚实的商业做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company used dishonest advertising to attract careless customers.  公司用不诚实的广告吸引不细心的顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 guileful</del></td><td>/ˈɡaɪlfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>狡诈的，欺骗的</td><td>a guileful negotiator<br>狡诈的谈判者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guileful merchant tricked several villagers out of their savings.  这个狡诈的商人骗走了几位村民的积蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 artless</td><td>/ˈɑrtləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏技巧的，笨拙的</td><td>an artless imitation<br>拙劣的模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painting was an artless copy of a famous portrait.  这幅画是对一幅名作的拙劣临摹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 guileless</td><td>/ˈɡaɪlləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单纯的，不狡诈的</td><td>a guileless smile<br>天真无邪的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her guileless smile made everyone in the room trust her immediately.  她天真无邪的笑容让屋里所有人立刻信任她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ingenuous</td><td>/ɪnˈdʒenjuəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>天真淳朴的</td><td>an ingenuous young student<br>天真淳朴的年轻学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His ingenuous questions about the city amused the veteran taxi driver greatly.  他关于这座城市的天真提问让老出租车司机大为好笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 innocent</td><td>/ˈɪnəsənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的，无恶意的；无辜的</td><td>an innocent remark<br>无恶意的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What began as an innocent joke ended in a serious quarrel.  一句本无恶意的玩笑最后引发了激烈争吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sincere</td><td>/sɪnˈsɪr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>真诚的，诚挚的</td><td>a sincere apology<br>真诚的道歉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her sincere apology repaired a friendship damaged for years.  她真诚的道歉修复了受损多年的友谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 honest</td><td>/ˈɑːnəst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>诚实的，正直的</td><td>an honest answer<br>一个诚实的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Give me an honest answer, even if it disappoints me.  给我一个诚实的答复，即使会让我失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 undesigning</del></td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈzaɪnɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无心机的，坦率的</td><td>an undesigning honest remark<br>毫无心机的诚实话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His undesigning honesty won friends wherever he happened to travel.  他毫无心机的诚实让他无论走到哪里都能交到朋友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>denounce</b></td><td>/dɪˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开指责</td><td>denounce corruption<br>公开指责腐败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Local leaders publicly denounced the corruption exposed by the newspaper.  当地领导人公开谴责报纸曝光的腐败行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 condemn</td><td>/kənˈdem/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谴责（…为不道德的、邪恶的）</td><td>condemn the attack<br>谴责这次袭击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 World leaders quickly condemned the attack on the hospital.  各国领导人迅速谴责了对医院的袭击。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 objurgate</del></td><td>/ˈɑːbdʒərɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉斥责，痛骂</td><td>objurgate the offender<br>痛斥违规者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old professor objurgated students who plagiarized their essays.  这位老教授严厉斥责抄袭论文的学生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rebuke</td><td>/rɪˈbjuːk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>斥责，责难</td><td>a sharp rebuke<br>严厉的斥责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge rebuked the lawyer for interrupting the witness.  法官斥责律师打断证人发言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reprimand</td><td>/ˈreprəˌmænd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>训斥，正式谴责</td><td>reprimand an employee<br>训斥员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was reprimanded for leaving his post early.  这名警官因提早离岗而受到训斥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reproach</td><td>/rɪˈproʊtʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>批评，责备</td><td>reproach oneself for<br>因某事自责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She reproached him for never returning her calls.  她责备他从不回她的电话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reprove</td><td>/rɪˈpruːv/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>温和地责备，警告</td><td>reprove a student mildly<br>温和地批评学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach reproved the player for arguing with the referee.  教练责备那名球员与裁判争辩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cite</td><td>/ˈsaɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>表扬，嘉奖；引用</td><td>cite a soldier for bravery<br>因勇敢嘉奖士兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general cited three soldiers for exceptional bravery under fire.  将军嘉奖三名在火线下表现极其勇敢的士兵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 commend</td><td>/kəˈmend/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞扬，表扬</td><td>commend her courage<br>赞扬她的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The principal commended her courage in front of the whole school.  校长在全校面前赞扬了她的勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 endorse</td><td>/ɪnˈdɔːrs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开支持</td><td>endorse a candidate<br>公开支持某候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union voted to endorse the mayor for a second term.  工会投票支持市长连任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 panegyric</td><td>/ˌpænəˈdʒɪrɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>赞颂之词，颂文</td><td>deliver a long panegyric<br>发表一篇长颂词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The retirement dinner ended with a long panegyric to the old professor.  退休晚宴以对这位老教授的长篇颂词结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accolade</td><td>/ˈækəleɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>赞扬，荣誉；奖励</td><td>win the highest accolade<br>赢得最高荣誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel received accolades from critics on both continents.  这部小说获得了两大洲评论家的赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 eulogy</td><td>/ˈjuːlədʒi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>悼词；颂词，赞辞</td><td>deliver a moving eulogy<br>致感人的悼词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She delivered a moving eulogy at her grandfather's funeral.  她在祖父的葬礼上致了感人的悼词。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>dent</b></td><td>/dent/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>凹痕，凹陷；使凹陷</td><td>a dent in the door<br>门上的一处凹痕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A small dent in the car door annoyed him for years.  车门上一个小凹痕让他好几年都很在意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abate</td><td>/əˈbeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>减轻，减弱；减少</td><td>the storm gradually abated<br>风暴逐渐减弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fever finally abated after two days of medication.  服药两天后，烧终于退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 downscale</td><td>/ˈdaʊnˌskeɪl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>缩减规模；低档的</td><td>downscale production<br>缩减生产规模</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company decided to downscale its overseas operations.  公司决定缩减海外业务规模。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 downsize</td><td>/ˈdaʊnˌsaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>缩减规模，裁员</td><td>downsize the workforce<br>裁减员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The firm downsized its workforce after losing two major contracts.  公司在丢掉两个大合同后裁员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dwindle</td><td>/ˈdwɪndl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>逐渐减少</td><td>dwindle to nothing<br>逐渐减少至无</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their savings dwindled during the months of unemployment.  失业的几个月里他们的积蓄逐渐减少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aggrandize</td><td>/əˈɡrændaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扩大（权力、财富、声望）</td><td>aggrandize one's power<br>扩张自己的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor used the crisis to aggrandize his own power.  市长利用危机扩大自己的权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amplify</td><td>/ˈæmpləˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扩大，增强；详述</td><td>amplify a signal<br>放大信号</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Could you amplify your last remark a little?  你能把最后那句话再详述一下吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 augment</td><td>/ɔɡˈment/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>增加，增强</td><td>augment one's income<br>增加收入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She augments her salary by teaching evening classes.  她通过教夜校课程增加收入。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 boost</td><td>/ˈbuːst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>提高，促进；推动</td><td>boost sales<br>促进销售</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax cut is expected to boost consumer spending.  减税预计会促进消费者支出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enlarge</td><td>/ɪnˈlɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>扩大，增大</td><td>enlarge the photograph<br>放大照片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They enlarged the kitchen by removing one interior wall.  他们拆掉一道内墙把厨房扩大了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 escalate</td><td>/ˈeskəleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）（战争等）升级，扩大</td><td>escalate into violence<br>升级为暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The border dispute quickly escalated into a full-scale armed conflict.  边界争端迅速升级为全面武装冲突。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>denude</b></del></td><td>/dɪˈnuːd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>脱去，使赤裸</td><td>denude the hillside of trees<br>使山坡树木尽失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long drought denuded the hillside of grass and young trees.  长期干旱使山坡上的草和幼树消失殆尽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cover</td><td>/ˈkʌvər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>掩盖，遮盖；覆盖物</td><td>cover up the truth<br>掩盖真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They tried to cover up the truth with a vague statement.  他们试图用含糊的声明掩盖真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 9

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>deplete</b></td><td>/dɪˈpliːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>耗尽，使衰竭</td><td>deplete natural resources<br>耗尽自然资源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long drought depleted the town's water reserves.  长期干旱耗尽了小镇的储水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consume</td><td>/kənˈsuːm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>消耗，耗尽</td><td>consume vast amounts of energy<br>消耗大量能源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Data centers consume vast amounts of electricity every single day.  数据中心每天消耗大量电力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 devour</td><td>/dɪˈvaʊər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>吞食，耗尽</td><td>devour the entire meal<br>吞掉整顿饭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hungry children devoured the entire meal in five minutes.  饥饿的孩子们五分钟内吞掉了整顿饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drain</td><td>/ˈdreɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>耗尽，使枯竭</td><td>drain the country's resources<br>耗尽国家资源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long war drained the country's resources and its morale.  漫长的战争耗尽了国家的资源和士气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exhaust</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>耗尽</td><td>exhaust the water supply<br>耗尽水源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two dry summers exhausted the town's entire water supply.  连续两个干燥的夏天耗尽了全镇的水源。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 draw down</td><td>/drɔː ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>消耗，动用（储备）</td><td>draw down the reserves<br>动用消耗储备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government drew down its oil reserves during the long crisis.  政府在漫长的危机期间动用了石油储备。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 play out</td><td>/pleɪ ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>耗尽，用完</td><td>play out its usefulness<br>用尽其价值</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old strategy had played out its usefulness by then.  到那时旧策略已经用尽了它的价值。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 use up</td><td>/juːz ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>用完，耗尽</td><td>use up the last supplies<br>用完最后的补给</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We used up the last supplies before reaching the base camp.  我们在到达大本营前用完了最后的补给。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enrich</td><td>/ɪnˈrɪtʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使丰富，增强</td><td>enrich the soil<br>使土壤肥沃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reading widely will enrich your understanding of other cultures.  广泛阅读会丰富你对其他文化的理解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 renew</td><td>/rɪˈnuː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>补充更新，续期</td><td>renew the water supply<br>补充水源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Spring rains renewed the reservoir after a long dry summer.  春雨在漫长干旱的夏季后补满了水库。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 replace</td><td>/rɪˈpleɪs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>补充，替换</td><td>replace the depleted stock<br>补充耗尽的库存</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The store quickly replaced the depleted stock of bottled water.  商店迅速补充了售空的瓶装水库存。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>deplore</b></td><td>/dɪˈplɔːr/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谴责，强烈反对</td><td>deplore the violence<br>谴责暴力行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Church leaders deplored the violence and called for immediate restraint.  教会领袖谴责暴力行为，呼吁立即克制。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blame</td><td>/ˈbleɪm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>责备，归咎于</td><td>blame someone for something<br>因某事责怪某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't blame me for the broken window.  别把窗户破了怪在我头上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 censure</td><td>/ˈsenʃər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>公开表示反对，谴责</td><td>a vote of censure<br>谴责性投票</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council voted to censure the mayor for his conduct.  议会投票谴责市长的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 criticize</td><td>/ˈkrɪtɪˌsaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>批评，指责；评论</td><td>criticize the decision<br>批评这个决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several members criticized the plan for ignoring small farmers.  几名成员批评该计划忽视了小农。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 denounce</td><td>/dɪˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开指责</td><td>denounce corruption<br>公开指责腐败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Local leaders publicly denounced the corruption exposed by the newspaper.  当地领导人公开谴责报纸曝光的腐败行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pan</td><td>/pæn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>严厉批评</td><td>pan a new film<br>严厉批评一部新片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics panned the film for its clumsy dialogue and predictable ending.  影评人严厉批评这部电影对话笨拙、结局可预料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 reprehend</del></td><td>/reprɪˈhend/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谴责，指责</td><td>reprehend such conduct<br>谴责这种行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee did not hesitate to reprehend the officer for his careless report.  委员会毫不犹豫地谴责了那名官员草率的报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extol</td><td>/ɪkˈstoʊl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美；吹捧</td><td>extol his virtues<br>赞美他的品德</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors still extol his kindness during the long winter flood.  邻居们至今仍赞美他在漫长冬季洪水期间的善举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 laud</td><td>/ˈlɔːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，称赞</td><td>laud his courage<br>赞扬他的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lauded the young pianist for her fearless interpretation.  评论家赞扬这位年轻钢琴家大胆的演绎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 praise</td><td>/ˈpreɪz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>赞扬，称赞</td><td>win high praise<br>获得高度赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won high praise from serious critics.  她的第一部小说赢得了严肃评论家的高度赞扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>deploy</b></td><td>/dɪˈplɔɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>（有目的地）展开；调度，部署</td><td>deploy troops along the border<br>沿边境部署军队</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The army deployed engineers to rebuild the bridges after the flood.  军队部署工兵在洪水过后重建桥梁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concentrate</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsntreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>浓缩；聚集</td><td>concentrate the solution<br>浓缩溶液</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Boil the syrup slowly in order to concentrate the solution.  慢慢熬煮糖浆以便浓缩溶液。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>deportation</b></td><td>/ˌdiːpɔːrˈteɪʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>驱逐出境，遣送</td><td>face deportation to his homeland<br>面临被遣送回国</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After years of appeals he still faced deportation to a country he barely knew.  上诉多年后，他仍面临被遣送到一个几乎陌生的国家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 banishment</td><td>/ˈbænɪʃmənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>流放，放逐</td><td>sentenced to permanent banishment<br>被判永久流放</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet endured years of banishment far from his native city.  这位诗人远离故城，忍受了多年的流放。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 displacement</td><td>/dɪsˈpleɪsmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>迁移，被迫离开家园</td><td>mass displacement of civilians<br>平民大规模被迫迁移</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The conflict caused the mass displacement of thousands of civilians.  冲突造成数千平民大规模流离失所。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expatriation</td><td>/ˌekspeɪtriˈeɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>移居国外；放逐</td><td>voluntary expatriation to Europe<br>自愿移居欧洲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His voluntary expatriation to Paris lasted almost thirty years.  他自愿移居巴黎将近三十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expulsion</td><td>/ɪkˈspʌlʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>驱逐，开除</td><td>expulsion from the school<br>被学校开除</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheating on the final exam led to his immediate expulsion.  期末考试作弊导致他被立即开除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relegation</td><td>/ˌrɛlɪˈɡeɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>贬降，放逐</td><td>relegation to a minor post<br>被贬到次要职位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His relegation to a minor post ended a promising career.  他被贬到次要职位，结束了前途看好的事业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>depose</b></td><td>/dɪˈpoʊz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>宣誓作证；废黜，罢免</td><td>depose the aging king<br>废黜年迈的国王</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rebel officers deposed the president and installed a temporary ruling council.  叛军军官废黜了总统，成立了临时统治委员会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attest</td><td>/əˈtest/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，为…作证</td><td>attest to his good character<br>证明他品行良好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several neighbors will attest to his good character in court.  几位邻居将在法庭上证明他品行良好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 swear</td><td>/swer/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>宣誓；咒骂</td><td>swear to tell the truth<br>宣誓说出真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Each witness must swear to tell the truth before testifying.  每位证人作证前必须宣誓说出真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 defrock</del></td><td>/diːˈfrɑːk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>剥夺神职</td><td>defrock a disgraced priest<br>剥夺失德神父的神职</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The church defrocked the priest after the lengthy investigation.  经过漫长调查后教会剥夺了这位神父的神职。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deprive</td><td>/dɪˈpraɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>剥夺，使丧失</td><td>deprive him of his rights<br>剥夺他的权利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new law deprived many residents of their voting rights.  新法剥夺了许多居民的投票权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 oust</td><td>/aʊst/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>驱逐</td><td>oust the corrupt mayor<br>赶走腐败的市长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board voted to oust the chairman after the scandal became public.  丑闻曝光后，董事会投票罢免了主席。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 uncrown</del></td><td>/ʌnˈkraʊn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>废黜，夺去王位</td><td>uncrown a hated monarch<br>废黜遭憎恨的君主</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rebellion uncrowned a monarch who had ruled for decades.  这场叛乱废黜了统治数十年的君主。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 perjure</td><td>/ˈpɜːrdʒər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>作伪证</td><td>perjure oneself in court<br>在法庭上作伪证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He perjured himself in court to protect his younger brother.  他在法庭上作伪证以保护弟弟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 crown</td><td>/ˈkraʊn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使达到顶点；王冠</td><td>crown a career<br>为职业生涯添彩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prize crowned a long career of quiet, patient research.  这个奖为漫长而耐心的研究生涯锦上添花。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enthrone</td><td>/ɪnˈθroʊn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使登王位，拥立</td><td>enthrone the young prince<br>拥立年轻王子登位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobles enthroned the young prince before his tenth birthday.  贵族们在小王子十岁前拥立他登位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>deposit</b></td><td>/dɪˈpɑːzɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>自然积累，沉积，矿藏；存钱</td><td>a deposit of coal<br>一处煤的矿藏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Geologists found a rich deposit of copper beneath the dry valley.  地质学家在干谷下面发现了丰富的铜矿藏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dregs</td><td>/ˈdreɡz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>渣滓，沉淀物</td><td>the dregs of the coffee<br>咖啡的渣滓</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He poured out the bitter dregs of the cold coffee.  他倒掉了冷咖啡里苦涩的渣滓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 precipitate</td><td>/prɪˈsɪpɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>匆忙的；促使，导致</td><td>a precipitate departure<br>匆忙的离去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His precipitate resignation surprised colleagues who expected a much longer transition.  他匆忙的辞职让原以为过渡期会长得多的同事大吃一惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sediment</td><td>/ˈsedəmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>沉淀物，沉积物</td><td>a layer of river sediment<br>一层河流沉积物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick sediment settled at the bottom of the old reservoir.  厚厚的沉积物沉在旧水库底部。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 settlings</del></td><td>/ˈsɛtlɪŋz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>沉淀物，渣滓</td><td>the settlings in the barrel<br>桶底的沉淀物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers scraped the settlings from the bottom of the barrel.  工人从桶底刮出沉淀物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hoard</td><td>/hɔːrd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>贮藏，秘藏</td><td>hoard food and water<br>贮藏食物和水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Squirrels hoard nuts in hollow trees long before the first frost.  松鼠早在初霜之前就把坚果贮藏在树洞里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reserve</td><td>/rɪˈzɜːrv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>储备，保留；预订</td><td>a reserve of cash<br>一笔现金储备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city keeps a reserve of water for dry summers.  该市为干旱的夏季保留一份储水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 process of eroding</td><td>/ˈprɑːsɛs əv ɪˈroʊdɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>侵蚀过程</td><td>the slow process of eroding<br>缓慢的侵蚀过程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cliff shows the slow process of eroding by seawater.  这处崖壁显示出海水缓慢侵蚀的过程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 withdraw</td><td>/wɪðˈdrɔː/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>取出（款项）；撤回，撤退</td><td>withdraw money from the bank<br>从银行取钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stopped at the bank to withdraw money before the long trip.  他在长途旅行前去银行取钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>depravity</b></td><td>/dɪˈprævəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>道德败坏</td><td>sink into utter depravity<br>堕入彻底的道德败坏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trial revealed a level of depravity that shocked the small town.  庭审揭露出的道德败坏程度震惊了这个小镇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debauchery</td><td>/dəˈbɔːtʃəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>放荡，纵欲</td><td>a life of debauchery<br>放荡的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wasted his inheritance on years of reckless debauchery.  他把遗产浪费在多年放荡纵欲上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 iniquitousness</del></td><td>/ɪˈnɪkwɪtəsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>邪恶；不公正</td><td>the iniquitousness of war<br>战争的罪恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reformers denounced the iniquitousness of the regime's secret prisons.  改革者谴责该政权秘密监狱的邪恶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 licentiousness</td><td>/laɪˈsɛnʃəsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>放荡；淫乱</td><td>moral licentiousness<br>道德上的放荡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel exposes the licentiousness of the aristocratic court.  这部小说揭露了贵族宫廷的放荡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perversion</td><td>/pərˈvɜːrʒən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>堕落；反常；曲解</td><td>a perversion of justice<br>对正义的曲解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such a ruling is a perversion of the law.  这样的裁决是对法律的曲解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 turpitude</td><td>/ˈtɜːrpətuːd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>卑鄙</td><td>moral turpitude<br>道德败坏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was dismissed for acts of moral turpitude.  他因道德败坏的行为被解职。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 dissoluteness</del></td><td>/ˈdɪsəluːtnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>放纵；生活淫荡</td><td>a life of dissoluteness<br>放纵的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of dissoluteness ruined both his health and his fortune.  多年的放纵毁了他的健康和财产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 morality</td><td>/məˈræləti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>道德；德行</td><td>public morality<br>公共道德</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scandal raised hard questions about morality in politics.  这桩丑闻引出了政治道德的难题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 virtue</td><td>/ˈvɜːrtʃu/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>美德，德行；优点</td><td>a life of virtue<br>有德行的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Patience is a virtue that few travelers keep at airports.  耐心是少有旅客能在机场保持的美德。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>deprecate</b></td><td>/ˈdeprəkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>不赞成，反对；贬低</td><td>deprecate his efforts<br>贬低他的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He tends to deprecate his own achievements whenever anyone praises them.  每当有人称赞，他总倾向于贬低自己的成就。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 discountenance</del></td><td>/dɪsˈkaʊntənəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>不赞成；使窘迫</td><td>discountenance such behavior<br>不赞成这种行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee discountenanced any further spending on the project.  委员会不赞成该项目再有任何支出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disesteem</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsɪˈstiːm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>轻视，不尊重</td><td>hold in disesteem<br>予以轻视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics held the popular novelist in open disesteem.  批评家公开轻视这位通俗小说家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disfavor</td><td>/dɪsˈfeɪvər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不赞成；失宠</td><td>fall into disfavor<br>失宠，不受待见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old method gradually fell into disfavor among researchers.  这种旧方法逐渐不受研究者青睐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frown</td><td>/ˈfraʊn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>不悦，不赞成；皱眉</td><td>frown on the practice<br>不赞成这种做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most universities frown on students sharing their assignments.  大多数大学不赞成学生互相分享作业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 belittle</td><td>/bɪˈlɪtəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低，轻视</td><td>belittle her achievements<br>贬低她的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't belittle her achievements just because she works quietly.  别因为她默默工作就贬低她的成就。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 denigrate</td><td>/ˈdenɪɡreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诋毁，污蔑</td><td>denigrate a rival<br>诋毁对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He never denigrates his opponents, even after losing badly.  即使惨败，他也从不诋毁对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dismiss</td><td>/dɪˈsmɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解散，打发走；解雇；不予考虑</td><td>dismiss the class<br>宣布下课</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher dismissed the class ten minutes ahead of time.  老师提前十分钟宣布下课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disparage</td><td>/dɪˈspærɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低；轻蔑地说</td><td>disparage someone's efforts<br>贬低某人的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not disparage her work simply because she is young.  不要仅仅因为她年轻就贬低她的工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cry down</td><td>/kraɪ ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，诋毁</td><td>cry down a rival<br>贬低对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials tried to cry down the report as mere gossip.  官员们试图把那份报告贬为闲言碎语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 approve</td><td>/əˈpruːv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>批准；赞成</td><td>approve the budget<br>批准预算</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee finally approved the plan after three long meetings.  委员会经过三次长会终于批准了该计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 favor</td><td>/ˈfeɪvər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>赞同，好评；恩惠</td><td>win public favor<br>赢得公众好评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal quickly won favor among the younger members.  这项提议很快赢得年轻成员的支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acclaim</td><td>/əˈkleɪm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>称赞</td><td>win critical acclaim<br>赢得评论界赞誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won wide critical acclaim across Europe and Asia.  她的第一部小说在欧洲和亚洲赢得了广泛的评论界赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 applaud</td><td>/əˈplɔːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>称赞，赞成；鼓掌</td><td>applaud the decision<br>赞成这一决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Teachers applauded the decision to reduce homework for young pupils.  老师们赞成给低年级学生减少作业的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exalt</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːlt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞扬；提升（地位）</td><td>exalt to the skies<br>推崇到极点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics exalt her early novels as masterpieces of the era.  评论家将她早期的小说赞为那个时代的杰作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extol</td><td>/ɪkˈstoʊl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美；吹捧</td><td>extol his virtues<br>赞美他的品德</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors still extol his kindness during the long winter flood.  邻居们至今仍赞美他在漫长冬季洪水期间的善举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glorify</td><td>/ˈɡlɔːrəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，美化</td><td>glorify war and violence<br>美化战争与暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics said the film glorified war instead of questioning it.  批评者说这部电影美化战争而非质疑它。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>depreciate</b></td><td>/dɪˈpriːʃieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低…的价值</td><td>depreciate his contribution<br>贬低他的贡献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not depreciate the value of patience in such long negotiations.  不要贬低耐心在这样漫长谈判中的价值。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cheapen</td><td>/ˈtʃiːpən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>降低价值；贬低</td><td>cheapen the brand<br>损害品牌价值</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Endless discounts cheapened the label's carefully built reputation.  没完没了的折扣贬损了该品牌精心建立的声誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 depress</td><td>/dɪˈpres/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>压低，使降低；使沮丧</td><td>depress house prices<br>压低房价</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A weak harvest can depress prices for the whole season.  歉收会压低整个季度的价格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 devalue</td><td>/diːˈvæljuː/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使贬值；贬低</td><td>devalue the currency<br>使货币贬值</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government devalued the peso to boost exports quickly.  政府让比索贬值以迅速刺激出口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 downgrade</td><td>/ˈdaʊnɡreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>降级，降低等级</td><td>downgrade the credit rating<br>下调信用评级</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Analysts downgraded the company after two weak quarterly reports.  两份疲弱季报后分析师下调了该公司评级。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mark down</td><td>/mɑrk ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>降价，调低价格</td><td>mark down the price<br>把价格调低</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The store will mark down winter coats after the holidays.  节后这家店会给冬季外套降价。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 appreciate</td><td>/əˈpriːʃieɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>增值，涨价；欣赏</td><td>appreciate in value<br>价值上涨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old farmhouse has appreciated considerably over the last decade.  这座老农舍在过去十年里大幅增值。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enhance</td><td>/ɪnˈhæns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提高，增强，提升</td><td>enhance the flavor<br>提升风味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A little salt greatly enhances the sweetness of the fruit.  少许盐能大大提升水果的甜味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 upgrade</td><td>/ˌʌpˈɡreɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>提升，升级</td><td>upgrade the equipment<br>升级设备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The airline upgraded her to business class without any charge.  航空公司免费把她升级到商务舱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mark up</td><td>/mɑrk ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>提高标价，加价</td><td>mark up the retail price<br>提高零售价</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dealers mark up rare records by three or four times.  经销商把稀有唱片加价三四倍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>depressed</b></td><td>/dɪˈprest/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不开心的，情绪不高的，消沉的</td><td>feel deeply depressed<br>感到十分消沉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She felt depressed for weeks after the letter of rejection arrived.  收到拒信之后，她消沉了好几个星期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crestfallen</td><td>/ˈkrestfɔːlən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>垂头丧气的，沮丧的</td><td>look utterly crestfallen<br>显得十分沮丧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He came home crestfallen after losing the final match by one point.  决赛以一分之差落败后，他垂头丧气地回了家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dejected</td><td>/dɪˈdʒektɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沮丧的，失望的</td><td>a dejected look<br>沮丧的神情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She left the interview dejected and sure she had failed.  她面试后神情沮丧，确信自己失败了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 despondent</td><td>/dɪˈspɑːndənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沮丧的，绝望的</td><td>grow despondent about work<br>对工作变得沮丧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After three rejections he became despondent about ever publishing.  三次被拒后他对出版感到绝望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gloomy</td><td>/ˈɡluːmi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的</td><td>a gloomy mood<br>忧郁的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew gloomy whenever the winter rain lasted more than a week.  每当冬雨连下一周以上，她就变得忧郁起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 low-spirited</td><td>/ˌloʊˈspɪrətɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>情绪低落的</td><td>a low-spirited mood<br>低落的情绪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rainy week left the whole team low-spirited.  连绵阴雨让整个团队情绪低落。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wretched</td><td>/ˈretʃɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极差的；沮丧的</td><td>wretched living conditions<br>恶劣的居住条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Families lived in wretched conditions beside the flooded riverbank.  一些家庭在泛滥的河岸边过着极差的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 blissful</td><td>/ˈblɪsfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极幸福的，快乐的</td><td>a blissful silence<br>幸福的宁静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They spent a blissful week beside the quiet mountain lake.  他们在宁静的山湖边度过了幸福的一周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 buoyant</td><td>/ˈbuːjənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>心情好的</td><td>in a buoyant mood<br>心情愉快</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He arrived in a buoyant mood after hearing the good news.  听到好消息后，他心情愉快地到了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gleeful</td><td>/ˈɡliːfəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>欢欣的，兴高采烈的</td><td>a gleeful grin<br>兴高采烈的笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were gleeful when the snow closed their school.  大雪停课时孩子们兴高采烈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 joyous</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>快乐的，欢乐的</td><td>a joyous occasion<br>欢乐的场合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village held a joyous festival after the autumn harvest.  村里在秋收后办了欢乐的节庆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 jubilant</td><td>/ˈdʒuːbɪlənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>喜悦的</td><td>a jubilant crowd<br>欢欣的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nominee gave a short speech before the jubilant crowd.  被提名人在欢欣鼓舞的人群前发表了简短讲话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 10

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>deprivation</b></td><td>/ˌdeprɪˈveɪʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>剥夺；匮乏</td><td>sleep deprivation<br>睡眠剥夺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of sleep deprivation left the young doctor exhausted and irritable.  多年的睡眠剥夺让这位年轻医生疲惫易怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fecundity</td><td>/fɪˈkʌndəti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>多产；肥沃，富饶</td><td>the fecundity of the soil<br>土壤的肥沃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delta is famous for the fecundity of its farmland.  这片三角洲以农田肥沃著称。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>deracinate</b></del></td><td>/ˌdiːˈræsɪneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>根除</td><td>deracinate old customs<br>根除旧习俗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Colonial policy sought to deracinate the island's native languages.  殖民政策企图根除岛上的本土语言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 plant</td><td>/ˈplænt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>种植，栽种；植物；工厂</td><td>plant a row of trees<br>栽种一排树</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Volunteers came to plant young oaks along the muddy riverbank.  志愿者来到泥泞的河岸栽种小橡树。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>derelict</b></td><td>/ˈderəlɪkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>玩忽职守的，不认真的</td><td>derelict in his duty<br>玩忽职守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guard was found derelict in his duty on the night of the theft.  那名警卫被认定在失窃当夜玩忽职守。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disregardful</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsrɪˈɡɑrdfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不顾的，漠视的</td><td>disregardful of safety<br>漠视安全</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The driver was disregardful of every posted speed limit.  这名司机漠视每一处限速标志。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lax</td><td>/læks/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>松懈的，不严格的；漫不经心的</td><td>lax security standards<br>松懈的安保标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lax security standards at the warehouse allowed the theft to go unnoticed.  仓库松懈的安保标准使这起盗窃未被发觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 neglectful</td><td>/nɪˈɡlektfəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>疏忽的，不留心的</td><td>neglectful of his duties<br>疏忽职责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report found the agency neglectful of basic safety inspections.  报告认定该机构疏忽了基本安全检查。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 neglecting</td><td>/nɪˈɡlektɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>忽视的，怠慢的</td><td>neglecting his studies<br>忽视学业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent the term gaming, neglecting every assignment he received.  他整学期打游戏，忽视了收到的每项作业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remiss</td><td>/rɪˈmɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>疏忽的，不留心的</td><td>be remiss in one's duties<br>疏于职守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I would be remiss if I did not thank the volunteers.  如果我不感谢志愿者，那就是我的疏忽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slack</td><td>/slæk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>松弛的；疏忽的，大意的</td><td>slack about deadlines<br>对截止日期松懈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager was slack about deadlines and lost several clients.  新经理对截止日期很松懈，因此丢了好几个客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extremely careful</td><td>/ɪkˈstriːmli ˈkɛrfəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极其小心的</td><td>extremely careful with money<br>用钱极其小心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Restorers must be extremely careful with such fragile old manuscripts.  修复者对这类脆弱的古手稿必须极其小心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 attentive</td><td>/əˈtentɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>留心的，专注的；周到的</td><td>an attentive audience<br>专注的听众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse stayed attentive to every change in his breathing.  护士留心他呼吸的每一点变化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conscientious</td><td>/ˌkɑːnʃiˈenʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>仔细的，一丝不苟的；有良心的，正直的</td><td>a conscientious worker<br>一位认真的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A conscientious editor checks every fact before the article goes to print.  一丝不苟的编辑会在文章付印前核实每一个事实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 nonnegligent</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnˈnɛɡlɪdʒənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无过失的，不疏忽的</td><td>nonnegligent conduct<br>无过失的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court ruled the pilot's actions entirely nonnegligent.  法院裁定飞行员的行为完全无过失。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>deride</b></td><td>/dɪˈraɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>嘲弄，嘲笑</td><td>deride the proposal<br>嘲笑该提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics derided the proposal as naive and completely out of touch.  批评者嘲笑该提案幼稚且完全脱离现实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gibe</td><td>/dʒaɪb/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>嘲弄</td><td>gibe at his accent<br>嘲弄他的口音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The other students gibed at his accent until he stopped speaking.  其他学生嘲弄他的口音，直到他不再开口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jeer</td><td>/ˈdʒɪr/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>嘲笑，讥讽</td><td>jeer at the loser<br>嘲笑失败者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd jeered at the player who missed the penalty.  人群嘲笑那名罚失点球的球员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mock</td><td>/ˈmɑːk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>假的，模拟的；嘲笑</td><td>a mock trial<br>模拟审判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students held a mock election to learn how voting works.  学生举行模拟选举以了解投票如何运作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scout</td><td>/ˈskaʊt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>嘲笑，轻蔑地拒绝；侦察</td><td>scout the suggestion<br>嘲笑该建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old captain scouted every warning the young sailors offered.  老船长嘲笑年轻水手提出的每句警告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shoot down</td><td>/ʃuːt ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>驳倒，否决；击落</td><td>shoot down the plan<br>否决该计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee shot down his budget request within ten minutes.  委员会十分钟内就否决了他的预算申请。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laugh at</td><td>/læf ˈæt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>嘲笑，取笑</td><td>laugh at his mistakes<br>嘲笑他的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobody dared laugh at the beginner's clumsy first attempt.  没人敢嘲笑新手笨拙的第一次尝试。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 praise</td><td>/ˈpreɪz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>赞扬，称赞</td><td>win high praise<br>获得高度赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel won high praise from serious critics.  她的第一部小说赢得了严肃评论家的高度赞扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 veneration</td><td>/ˌvenəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>崇敬，敬仰</td><td>an object of veneration<br>崇敬的对象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ancestors are held in veneration throughout much of East Asia.  在东亚许多地区，祖先受到崇敬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>derivative</b></td><td>/dɪˈrɪvətɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非原创的</td><td>a derivative style<br>缺乏原创的风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reviewers called the album pleasant but derivative of earlier folk records.  评论者称这张专辑悦耳，却是对早期民谣唱片的模仿而无原创。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 secondhand</td><td>/ˈsekəndˌhænd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>二手的；间接得来的</td><td>secondhand information<br>间接得来的消息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most of his knowledge of the war was secondhand.  他对那场战争的了解多是间接得来的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 original</td><td>/əˈrɪdʒənl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>创新的；最初的，最早的</td><td>an original idea<br>有创意的想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her proposal was genuinely original, unlike the tired ideas we usually hear.  她的提案确实新颖，不像我们常听到的那些陈旧想法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
