<tr><td>↳反 innovative</td><td>/ˈɪnəveɪtɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>创新性的</td><td>innovative design<br>创新的设计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The firm survived the crisis thanks to an innovative approach to distribution.  这家公司靠创新的分销方式度过了危机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 precursory</td><td>/prɪˈkɜrsəri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>先前的；预示的</td><td>precursory signs of illness<br>疾病的前兆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Faint tremors were precursory to the eruption that followed.  轻微的震动预示了随后的火山喷发。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>descend</b></td><td>/dɪˈsend/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>下降；世代相传</td><td>descend from the summit<br>从山顶下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The path descends steeply toward the river through thick pine forest.  小径穿过浓密的松林，向河边陡然下降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dip</td><td>/ˈdɪp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>下降，下沉；蘸</td><td>a dip in sales<br>销量的下滑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Temperatures dip sharply once the sun sets over the desert.  沙漠上日落后气温骤降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fall</td><td>/ˈfɔːl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>下降，落下；秋天</td><td>a sharp fall in prices<br>价格急剧下跌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Share prices fell again after the disappointing earnings report.  令人失望的财报公布后股价再次下跌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plunge</td><td>/plʌndʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>突然下降</td><td>plunge into the valley<br>骤降到谷底</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The road plunges into the valley just beyond the last farmhouse.  过了最后一座农舍，道路便骤降进山谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sink</td><td>/ˈsɪŋk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>下沉，下降；水槽</td><td>sink below the horizon<br>沉到地平线下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old boat began to sink soon after the storm.  暴风雨过后不久那条旧船开始下沉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 arise</td><td>/əˈraɪz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>出现，产生；升起</td><td>problems arise<br>问题出现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New questions arise whenever the committee reviews these old files.  委员会每次复审这些旧档案都会出现新问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ascend</td><td>/əˈsend/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>上升，攀登</td><td>ascend the staircase<br>登上楼梯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The balloon ascended slowly above the quiet morning fields.  热气球在清晨宁静的田野上缓缓上升。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 climb</td><td>/ˈklaɪm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>攀登，上升</td><td>climb a steep hill<br>爬上陡坡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We climbed for hours before reaching the windy stone ridge.  我们爬了几个小时才到多风的石岭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mount</td><td>/ˈmaʊnt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>增长，上升</td><td>tension mounts<br>紧张气氛加剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pressure on the minister mounted as the scandal spread.  随着丑闻扩散，部长承受的压力不断增加。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 upsweep</del></td><td>/ˈʌpswiːp/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>向上弯曲；上升趋势</td><td>the upsweep of the roofline<br>屋顶线的上扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The architect admired the graceful upsweep of the temple eaves.  建筑师赞叹寺庙屋檐优美的上扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 upturn</td><td>/ˈʌptɜːrn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>好转，回升；使朝上</td><td>an upturn in the economy<br>经济的回升</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Retailers finally reported an upturn in holiday spending.  零售商终于报告节日消费出现回升。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 forbears</td><td>/ˈfɔrbɛrz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>祖先，先辈</td><td>the customs of our forbears<br>我们先辈的习俗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her forbears farmed this narrow valley for nearly two centuries.  她的祖先在这条狭窄山谷耕作了近两百年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>divination</b></td><td>/ˌdɪvɪˈneɪʃn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>占卜，预言</td><td>the art of divination<br>占卜之术</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ancient priests practiced the art of divination by reading cracks in burnt bones.  古代祭司通过解读烧裂的骨纹来施行占卜之术。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 augury</td><td>/ˈɔɡjəri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>预兆；占卜</td><td>a good augury for the future<br>未来的好预兆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clear sunrise was taken as a favorable augury.  晴朗的日出被视为吉兆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forecast</td><td>/ˈfɔːrˌkæst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>预测，预报</td><td>forecast heavy rain<br>预报大雨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Economists forecast slower growth for the coming two years.  经济学家预测未来两年增长会放缓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 predictions</td><td>/priˈdɪkʃənz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>预言，预测</td><td>make accurate predictions<br>做出准确的预测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her predictions about the election proved surprisingly accurate.  她对选举的预测出乎意料地准确。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>desecrate</b></td><td>/ˈdesɪkreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>亵渎，玷污</td><td>desecrate a holy shrine<br>亵渎神圣的殿堂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vandals desecrated the old cemetery and overturned a dozen stones.  破坏者亵渎了那座老墓园，推倒了十几块墓碑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defile</td><td>/dɪˈfaɪl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>玷污，污损；亵渎</td><td>defile a sacred site<br>亵渎圣地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vandals defiled the ancient temple with paint and broken glass.  破坏者用油漆和碎玻璃亵渎了那座古庙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 profane</td><td>/prəˈfeɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>亵渎</td><td>profane a sacred place<br>亵渎圣地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vandals profaned the temple by scrawling slogans on its walls.  破坏者在庙墙上乱涂标语，亵渎了圣殿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 violate</td><td>/ˈvaɪəleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>违反；侵犯；亵渎</td><td>violate a sacred grave<br>亵渎神圣的坟墓</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Looters violated the tombs that villagers had guarded for centuries.  盗墓者亵渎了村民守护数百年的墓葬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sanctify</td><td>/ˈsæŋktɪfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使神圣，使圣化</td><td>sanctify the temple ground<br>使庙地成为圣地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bishop came to sanctify the ground where the chapel would stand.  主教前来使这片将建礼拜堂的土地成为圣地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 revere</td><td>/rɪˈvɪr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（尤指对神的）尊崇，尊敬</td><td>revered as a hero<br>被尊崇为英雄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students in the village still revere the teacher who founded their school.  村里的学生仍然尊崇创办他们学校的那位老师。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hallow</td><td>/ˈhæloʊ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>尊敬，把…视为神圣</td><td>hallow the ancient site<br>将古址奉为神圣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Generations of pilgrims have hallowed this quiet mountain spring.  世代朝圣者把这泓山泉视为神圣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>desiccate</b></del></td><td>/ˈdesɪkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使干燥，使脱水</td><td>desiccate the soil completely<br>使土壤完全干透</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months without rain desiccated the fields and killed the young corn.  数月无雨使田地干透，幼小的玉米也枯死了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 castrate</td><td>/ˈkæsˌtreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阉割；使失去活力</td><td>castrate the argument<br>削弱论点的力量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Editors castrated the essay by cutting its sharpest passages.  编辑删去最犀利的段落，使文章失去了力量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dampen</td><td>/ˈdæmpən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制，压抑（感情、精力等），泼冷水</td><td>dampen his enthusiasm<br>打击他的热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Losing the first game did nothing to dampen the team's spirits.  首场失利丝毫没有打击球队的士气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deaden</td><td>/ˈdedən/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使减弱，使麻木</td><td>deaden the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick carpets deaden the sound of footsteps upstairs.  厚地毯减弱了楼上的脚步声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 devitalize</del></td><td>/diːˈvaɪtəlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使失去活力</td><td>devitalize the local economy<br>使当地经济失去活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of neglect devitalized the once thriving downtown district.  多年的忽视使曾经繁荣的市中心失去活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 enervate</del></td><td>/ˈenərveɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>enervate the whole team<br>使全队疲弱无力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long heat wave enervated the whole team before the final match.  漫长的热浪在决赛前使全队疲弱无力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 brace</td><td>/ˈbreɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使振作，鼓励；支撑</td><td>brace yourself for impact<br>做好承受冲击准备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cup of hot tea braced him for the long night.  一杯热茶让他为漫长的夜晚振作起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 energize</td><td>/ˈenərdʒaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使充满活力，激励</td><td>energize the whole team<br>使整个团队振奋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new coach energized a team that had lost hope.  新教练让失去希望的球队重新振奋起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enliven</td><td>/ɪnˈlaɪvn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使活跃，使生动</td><td>enliven a dull party<br>让沉闷的聚会活跃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her jokes enlivened an otherwise dull afternoon meeting.  她的玩笑让本来沉闷的下午会议活跃起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 invigorate</td><td>/ɪnˈvɪɡəreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使精神，使强壮，鼓舞激励</td><td>invigorate the local economy<br>振兴当地经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk in cold air always invigorates my tired mind.  在冷空气中短暂散步总能让我疲惫的头脑重新振作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stimulate</td><td>/ˈstɪmjəˌleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刺激，激发；促进</td><td>stimulate economic growth<br>促进经济增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Warm rain stimulated the seeds to sprout within a week.  温暖的雨水刺激种子一周内发芽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vitalize</td><td>/ˈvaɪtəlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赋予活力，激励</td><td>vitalize the local economy<br>使当地经济充满活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New investment vitalized the sleepy town within just two years.  新投资在两年内使这个沉寂的小镇焕发活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>designate</b></td><td>/ˈdezɪɡneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>任命；命名</td><td>designate an heir<br>指定继承人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council designated the whole valley a protected natural area.  议会将整个山谷指定为自然保护区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assign</td><td>/əˈsaɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>指派，分配；指定</td><td>assign a difficult task<br>指派一项难任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor assigned the story to her most careful reporter.  编辑把这篇报道指派给最细心的记者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commission</td><td>/kəˈmɪʃən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>委任，委托；委员会</td><td>commission a new portrait<br>委托绘制新肖像</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city commissioned a young sculptor to design the memorial.  市政府委托一位年轻雕塑家设计纪念碑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 constitute</td><td>/ˈkɑːnstətuːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>指派，设立；构成</td><td>constitute a committee<br>设立委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board was properly constituted under the new rules.  依据新规则，董事会得以正式组成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nominate</td><td>/ˈnɑːmɪneɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提名，推荐；任命</td><td>nominate a candidate<br>提名一位候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The staff nominated her for the annual teaching award.  同事们提名她角逐年度教学奖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 place</td><td>/ˈpleɪs/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提出（问题）；放置</td><td>place the issue before them<br>把问题提交给他们</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She placed the question before the committee that morning.  那天上午她把这个问题提交给了委员会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 denominate</del></td><td>/dɪˈnɑːməˌneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>命名，取名</td><td>denominate the new species<br>为新物种命名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Botanists denominated the flower after the valley where it grew.  植物学家以花生长的山谷为它命名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dub</td><td>/dʌb/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>称…为；起绰号；配音</td><td>dub him a hero<br>称他为英雄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The papers dubbed the young pitcher the city's new hope.  报纸称这位年轻投手为全城的新希望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entitle</td><td>/ɪnˈtaɪtəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>给…命名；使有权利</td><td>entitle the book differently<br>给书另起书名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He entitled his memoir after a song from his childhood.  他用童年的一首歌为回忆录命名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 label</td><td>/ˈleɪbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>把…称为，标明；标签</td><td>label him a radical<br>把他称为激进分子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics labeled the film propaganda before anyone had watched it.  批评者在无人观看前就把影片称为宣传品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 style</td><td>/ˈstaɪl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>称…为，命名；风格</td><td>style himself a doctor<br>自称为医生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He styled himself a professor although he never finished college.  他自称教授，尽管从未读完大学。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 term</td><td>/ˈtɜːrm/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>把…称为；术语；学期</td><td>term it a failure<br>把它称为失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Economists termed the sudden slowdown a mild technical recession.  经济学家把这次骤缓称为轻度技术性衰退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 title</td><td>/ˈtaɪtəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>给…加标题；标题，头衔</td><td>title the painting simply<br>为画作简单命名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She titled the final chapter after her grandmother's village.  她以祖母的村子为最后一章命名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discharge</td><td>/dɪsˈtʃɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>释放；解雇</td><td>discharge the prisoner<br>释放囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court discharged the prisoner immediately after the new evidence appeared.  新证据出现后，法庭立即释放了那名囚犯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dismiss</td><td>/dɪˈsmɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解散，打发走；解雇；不予考虑</td><td>dismiss the class<br>宣布下课</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher dismissed the class ten minutes ahead of time.  老师提前十分钟宣布下课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 expel</td><td>/ɪkˈspel/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>驱逐；开除</td><td>expel a student<br>开除一名学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school expelled two students for cheating on the exam.  学校因考试作弊开除了两名学生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fire</td><td>/ˈfaɪər/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>解雇；开火；火</td><td>fire an idle worker<br>解雇懒散的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager fired two clerks after the audit found losses.  审计查出亏损后经理解雇了两名职员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>
